市場焦點
美國經濟
• 第三季GDP初值季增年率為3.4%，高於市場估計的3.2%(前季GDP為2.2%)。美國9月個人消
費支出(PCE)物價指數月增0.5%，年增3.6%；核心PCE指數月增0.3%、年增3.1%。
• 美國新一輪總體經濟數據偏向支撐股市宏觀多頭格局，然而中東衝突持續陷入僵局、市場對
於AI投資前景的疑慮持續，可能加大短期股市波動風險。考慮到當前市場風險與機會並存，
持股建議聚焦大型藍籌股，並適度多元分散產業布局。
期中選舉
• 美國期中選舉將於11月3日登場，若民主黨成功奪回眾議院，並進一步取得參議院多數席次，
將提高川普後續推動政策的難度。觀察過去美國總統四年任期的選舉週期，期中選舉的隔年
通常是股市表現最佳的年度，其背後可能反映期中選舉落幕後，政治不確定性降低，市場對

政策方向的預期逐漸明朗，且政府可能透過財政或經濟政策支撐成長。
中國
• 9月工業企業利潤年成長14.4%，增速較8月的4.2%明顯加快，中國工業利潤改善，主要由AI
硬體與能源價格上漲帶動，其中IT設備和有色金屬為AI的兩大受益行業，化學製品企業則是能
源價格上漲的最大受益者之一，短期而言AI、高技術製造與能源產業仍是市場主線，但內需疲
弱與價格競爭問題未解，投資上仍應以大型龍頭企業為主。

美國第三季GDP成長超預期、通膨壓力續增
• 美國第三季經濟成長率(GDP)創一年來最大增幅：美國經濟分析局(BEA)公布，第三季GDP初值季增年率為3.4%，高於市場
估計的3.2%(前季GDP為2.2%)。企業AI投資火熱、消費者開支保持韌性，抵銷了油價上漲衝擊。
• 9月數據顯示通膨壓力續增，但符合預期：美國9月個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數月增0.5%，寫3月以來最大月增幅；年增幅
3.6%。剔除食物與能源的9月核心PCE指數，月增0.3%、年增3.1%。
• 市場反應：由於新一輪通膨數據符合預期，市場未改變聯準會12月利率決議升息一碼的預測。然而，有跡象顯示實質個人
消費支出開始轉弱，儲蓄率也有所下降，市場開始押注通膨可能已經見頂。從聯邦利率期貨市場數據來看，市場對於明年升

息空間的預期已較上個月收斂(九月底預測升息2~3碼→目前預測1~2碼)，
• 觀點：美國新一輪總體經濟數據偏向支撐股市宏觀多頭格局，然而中東衝突持續陷入僵局、市場對於AI投資前景的疑慮持續，
可能加大短期股市波動風險。考慮到當前市場風險與機會並存，持股建議聚焦大型藍籌股，並適度多元分散產業布局。
資料來源：Bloomberg，兆豐銀行整理，2026.10.30。

聯準會貨幣決議與聲明稿差異
資料來源：Federal Reserve（10/28、9/16 聲明）；鉅亨網，2026/10/29。以上為聲明重點整理。
2 / 5
10月決議｜按兵不動
3.75% – 4.00%
10票贊成、2票反對
經濟措辭轉強 「穩健擴張」改為「強勁擴張」；國內支出具韌性， 生產力與資本投資表現強。
通膨仍未解除 聲明維持「通膨居高」的判斷，未再宣稱本次升息有 助加快回到2%目標。
政策訊號更具彈性 改稱需要更多資訊判斷適當政策立場，未承諾下一步 升息或降息。
關鍵變化
理事庫克(Lisa D. Cook) 和克里夫蘭聯準銀行總裁哈瑪克(Beth M. Hammack)主張本次升息1碼，
形成2張鷹派反對票；9月決議則為一致通過。利率不變不等於轉向寬鬆，委員會內部對通膨風險仍

有不同判斷。
市場解讀：本次是「暫停觀察」；12月是否再升息，仍取決於通膨、需求與能源價格。

聯準會會後記者會問答重點整理
資料來源：Federal Reserve 10/28會後記者會；Reuters、Investing.com，2026/10/28。
3 / 5
01｜市場利率反映緊縮壓力
華許指出，9月與10月兩次會議之
間，美國公債名目與實質殖利率明
顯走高；市場利率可先行反映成長
、通膨與風險變化，不必等政策利
率調整。
政策含意
長天期殖利率仍受通膨預期、期限
溢酬及公債供需影響；政策利率不
變，不代表債市壓力解除。
02｜AI投資與生產力
華許認為美國經濟尚未過熱，AI投
資推升需求，但生產力也有改善；
聯準會將持續評估新科技對產出、
就業與物價的中長期影響。
政策含意
AI支出可支撐企業成長，也伴隨資本
支出回收與融資風險；應檢視現金流
、投資報酬及生產力是否實際提升。

03｜政策制度與工具再檢視
聯準會規劃2027年安排1次、2028
年安排2次專注貨幣政策策略的會
議，不進行利率表決或發布政策聲
明；另將檢視資產負債表政策。
政策含意
制度調整不等於政策轉向寬鬆；仍須
觀察準備金、資產負債表操作及經濟
數據，評估金融條件如何變化。
解讀：華許除談利率外，也聚焦市場利率、AI生產力及政策制度調整；投資判斷仍應回到數據、企業獲利與金融條件。

聯準會利率決議摘要暨投資策略建議
資料來源：Federal Reserve；Reuters、InvestingLive；鉅亨網，2026/10/29。市場價格可能隨後續交易更新。
4 / 5
政策決議 利率維持 3.75% – 4.00% 經濟強、通膨高；政策下一步保留彈性
美股
短線受利率預期與科技股評價牽動
；AI投資支撐獲利，但仍需檢視現
金流與資本支出回報。
美債
若升息預期升溫，短天期殖利率較
敏感；長天期亦受通膨預期、發債
供給與期限溢酬影響。
美元／黃金
美元可能隨利差預期波動；黃金同
時受實質利率、美元及地緣政治避
險需求影響。
投資策略建議｜情境式配置
股票 保留核心持股，避免追價；優先關注獲利能見度、現金流與定價能力較佳企業。
債券 以分散到期日與信用風險為主；可分批配置高品質債券，避免單押長天期利率下行。

多元資產 保留流動性與再平衡空間；若通膨再升、殖利率上行，分批進場並控管整體波動。
後續觀察：美國通膨與就業數據、能源價格、企業財報，以及市場對12月政策的重新定價。

美國期中選舉登場，國會席次變化牽動川普施政
資料來源： 各媒體，2002~2022年
歷屆期中選舉確認結果時間
選舉日期 眾議院控制 權確認時間 參議院控制權 確認時間 時長
2006年11月7日 選舉日當晚 11月9日 2天
2010年11月2日 選舉日當晚 11月3日 1天
2014年11月4日 選舉日當晚 選舉日當晚 0天
2018年11月6日 選舉日當晚 選舉日當晚 0天
2022年11月8日 11月16日 11月12日 8天
• 美國期中選舉登場，國會席次變化牽動川普施政：美國期中選舉將於11月3日登場，為川普第二任期上任以來首場全國性選
舉，除國會議員改選外，亦包括州長及其他公職人員選舉。若民主黨成功奪回眾議院，並進一步取得參議院多數席次，將提
高川普後續推動政策的難度。不過，川普仍可透過發布行政命令等方式，在一定程度上繞過國會的立法制衡，持續推動其政

策議程。
• 本次選舉結果可能耗費一週才能明朗：回顧2006至2022年近五屆美國期中選舉，其中四屆的參眾兩院控制權於投票當晚或
隔日即大致明朗，僅2022年因關鍵席次競爭激烈、郵寄選票計票耗時較長，直至投票後第8天才確認兩院席次歸屬。考量本
次參議院選情膠著，加上川普執政以來美國政治極化持續加劇，不排除本次選舉結果需耗費一週才能明朗，期間市場也可能
面臨額外的不確定性。

期中選舉的隔年通常是股市表現最佳的年度
• 期中選舉的隔年通常是股市表現最佳的年度：觀察過去美國總統四年任期的選舉週期，期中選舉的隔年通常是股市表現最佳
的年度。根據1940至2025年統計，標普500指數第三年平均報酬率達16.5%，高於第一年的8.3%、第二年的4.2%及第四年
的6.9%；中位數報酬率更達18.9%。其背後可能反映期中選舉落幕後，政治不確定性降低，市場對政策方向的預期逐漸明朗，
且政府可能透過財政或經濟政策支撐成長。
• 政黨與國會組成影響有限，基本面仍是關鍵： 進一步觀察政黨差異，1940年以來，第三年由共和黨總統執政時，標普500指
數平均報酬率約18.2%，高於民主黨總統執政時的14.9%，但兩者均呈現正報酬，顯示總統所屬政黨並非決定股市表現的唯

一因素。國會組成亦可能影響政策推動與市場預期，例如共和黨總統搭配民主黨國會時，第三年平均報酬率約15.6%；若國
會呈現混合執政，平均報酬率則達23.1%。

能源與AI價格推動中國工業利潤增速回升
• 能源與AI價格推動中國工業利潤增速回升：9月工業企業利潤年成長14.4%，增速較8月的4.2%明顯加快。但前三季度累計增速
從1到8月的15.7%小幅放緩至15.5%，利潤改善主要反映了能源和AI硬體價格的上漲，其中AI硬體價格仍是高技術製造業利潤
的重要支撐。前三季度，高技術製造業利潤年成長52.0%，與1到8月54.7%的增速大體相當。拉動整體利潤成長約10個百分點，
貢獻率約三分之二。值得注意的是，利潤成長仍主要集中於少數相關行業，其他企業部門整體依然處境艱難，內需疲弱疊加價
格競爭激烈令利潤受到擠壓。
• 投資建議：中國工業利潤改善，主要由AI硬體與能源價格上漲帶動，其中IT設備和有色金屬為AI的兩大受益行業，化學製品企

業則是能源價格上漲的最大受益者之一，短期而言AI、高技術製造與能源產業仍是市場主線，但內需疲弱與價格競爭問題未解，
投資上仍應以大型龍頭企業為主。
資料來源：彭博，2026.10.27
能源、AI價格飆升提振中國相關行業利潤增速

中國科技公司享受負殖利率差
• 中國科技公司享受負殖利率差：中國在經濟成長放緩與寬鬆貨幣
政策下，借貸成本持續下降，10年期公債殖利率由年初1.86%降
至1.63%，使企業融資條件更加寬鬆，相較之下，美國受經濟韌
性與高利率政策影響，10年期公債殖利率升至約5%，企業整體融
資成本明顯提高。此外2025年中國大型科技企業在境內債券市場
融資規模有限，僅為約74億美元，而且支付的殖利率，平均比同
風 險 層 級 的 債 券 高 出18個 基 點，但現在溢 價 已 經 變 為 折 價 ，
2026年迄今，中國科技企業已在境內發債約100億美元，新發科
技債殖利率平均折價6個基點，顯示機構投資者信心發生了顯著轉
變，大幅降低了行業推進AI建設的資金成本，會有上述現象是由
於中國經濟通縮，難以承受更高利率，因此官方須維持較寬鬆的

金融條件，科技企業剛剛開始通過債券市場融資，預料債券將受
到投資者關注。
• 展望後市：低成本債券融資對中國科技行業構成利好，與IPO不
同，債券市場為企業提供了一個可重複利用的融資渠道，在股市
劇烈波動之際，對投資人是另一種資產配置，可優先關注具資金
優勢與應用場景的大型科技龍頭，以及受惠於AI算力需求成長的
伺服器、網通設備和資料中心產業。
中國科技公司享受負殖利率差
中美國債走勢分化
資料來源：彭博，2026.10.29

AI產業鏈融資警訊：資金缺口正在擴散
• 資本支出與現金流的嚴重失衡：AI生態系中的巨額投資正考驗著企業，一旦企業營運現金流不足以支持基礎設施建設，就必
須高度依賴外部融資(發債或增資)，使其更容易受到資本市場波動與利率變化的衝擊。AI算力提供商資金缺口最為顯著，其
資本支出已接近營運現金流3倍(284%)，需要投入大額資金採購高端GPU並對外出租算力，數據中心資本支出佔現金流的比
例已從2022年11月(ChatGPT問世)1倍(104%)快速爬升至1.3倍(128%)，超大規模雲服務商資本支出占現金流比例已從2022
年底40%翻倍飆升至73%。在彭博研究統計的8家超大型雲業者中，出現自由現金流為負(資金缺口)公司數量已從2022年的
1家增加至4家(達50%)。
• 產業鏈錯綜複雜，風險具連鎖效應：從上游晶片製造商、硬體開發商、建築商、電力/公用事業，到中游的數據中心與雲端

服務商，再到下游的軟體與數據管理公司，其銷售與支付合約彼此高度緊密相連。當算力提供商或數據中心出現資金吃緊時，
風險會迅速向上游傳遞，導致項目延期、融資成本攀升，甚至延後整個生態系的收入實現時程。目前除了雲端與數據中心外，
硬體開發商與建築商中出現資金缺口的企業比例也在上升。
資金缺口正蔓延至人工智能產業鏈一半的層級
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.28
算力提供商和數據中心的資本支出超過現金流

市場定價轉變：借貸成本上升與股票估值修正
• 資本市場已經開始針對資金缺口進行風險定價：在資金缺口最大的AI算力提供商、數據中心以及超大規模雲服務商中，其發
行的公司債相較於美國國債的殖利率差(利差)顯著拓寬，高於一般投資級債券或高收益債券的表現，顯示債券市場對其違約
與融資風險要求更高的風險補償。由於大量現金流被基礎設施建設掏空，且AI應用端的獲利回收速度未達市場預期，投資人
不再願意給予極高溢價，整體AI產業鏈的預期本益比出現明顯修正。
• 小結與投資建議：當前AI產業正在經歷基礎設施過渡期，資本密集的算力出租、數據中心建設正遭受強烈的資金擠壓，若未
來1~2年內下游應用端的商業化營收無法順利銜接，依賴高度槓桿與外部融資的企業將面臨嚴峻的流動性挑戰。對AI算力出
租商及部分高負債的數據中心開發商保持謹慎態度，一旦信貸市場收緊或借貸成本升高，利潤與營運將受重創。晶片製造商

的經營現金流持續成長，財務結構改善，選擇擁有強大訂價權與現金流護城河的上游半導體巨頭，安全邊際最高，電力與公
用事業資本支出佔現金流比例相對穩定且受惠於剛性需求。超大規模雲服務商中表現差異大，優先選擇資產負債表健全、資
產回報率高且擁有傳統核心業務能持續提供穩定現金流的科技巨頭，遠離需大量靠發債維持資本支出的個股。
超大規模雲服務商和數據中心的利差擴大
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.28
隨著借貸成本波動加劇，AI股權估值走弱

顧 • 利率可能維持高檔更久，但收益價值正在浮現：近月以
來美國10年期公債殖利率一度升抵5.36%，30年期接
近5.7%，反映市場重新評價未來利率中樞、財政赤字
及資金需求等因素。雖然長天期債券價格仍可能受到利
率波動影響，但高殖利率已大幅提高新進場資金的收益
起點，投資焦點應由預測利率轉向評估收益價值。
• 債券標售與資金流顯示配置需求逐步回溫：10月美國
財政部10年期、20年期及30年期公債標售需求維持穩
健，投標倍數約介於2.5至2.8倍區間；同時，美國債券
基金已連續26週呈現資金淨流入，顯示市場未必認為
利率已見頂，但已開始接受5%以上殖利率的長期配置
價值。
• 收益優先取代押注利率轉折：在Higher for Longer環
境下，債券的核心功能重新回到穩定收益、分散風險與

降低組合波動。建議可以3至7年期美國公債及高評等
投資級公司債作為核心收益部位，長天期債券則採分批
布局方式，兼顧收益來源及未來利率循環變化帶來的潛
在機會。

7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良

8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。
11. 屬基金資訊觀測站ESG基金專區所列之基金，有關基金之ESG資訊，投資人應於申購前詳閱基金公開說明書或投資人須知所載之基金所有特色或目標等資訊。

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

陳玫芬 合格證券投資分析人員 & CFP 
Lisa.mf.chen@yuanta.com 
陳亮齊 
chrischenlc@yuanta.com

InP 基板缺貨影響光通訊，3Q26 營運低於預期且 4Q 估計下修 
3Q26 營收 3.2 億元，季增 25%且年增 27%；毛利率/營益率為 39%/18%，
季增 4.7 ppts/9.7 ppts，EPS 1.26 元，季增 214%/年增 68%，低於預期 23%；
光通訊占比為 25%，低於 1Q26/2Q26 的 45%/37%。光通訊比重低使毛利率
較原估計低 3.9 ppts。估計 4Q26 營收 3.77 億元，季增 18% (10 月營收已達
1.24 億元)，下修 14%，主因 InP 基板持續缺貨中；毛利率下修 4.4ppts 至
38.8%；EPS 估計為 1.31 元，下修 38%。 
GaAs 應用之研發案增加，包含 VCSEL、QD Laser 及 PIN 
IET-KY 的 MBE 磊晶包含 GaAs(砷化鎵)、InP(磷化銦)、GaSb(銻化鎵)等材
料，雖然 InP 現階段基板不足，惟 GaAs 的研發案近期有所斬獲，如(1) 射頻
用於國防；(2) VCSEL，200G 射頻用於光通訊(市場尚未有量產者)；(3) 
QD(量子點)雷射，發射端已於 2026 年中送樣再調整結構中；(4) PIN，用於
光通訊的接收端。此外，GaSb(銻化鎵)估計 2026 年增 65%且占比 19%以國
防應用為主，近期亦有新的商業需求正與客戶洽談中。 
2026 及 2027 年 EPS 估計下修 31%及 29%，目前評價合理 
IET-KY 目前光通訊出貨以 100G 及 200G 的 PD 為主；公司無採用上游中國
大陸設廠之 AXT 的 InP 基板，目前採日系基板為主且今年處於即到貨即用的
緊俏狀態。德系供應商期待後續產能提升但仍需時間，本中心估計 2026 年
光通訊占比由 2025 年的 44%降至 35%，2027 年回到占比 44%。本中心下
修估計 2026/2027 年營收 13%/13%至 12.85 億/18.61 億元，年增 19%/45%，
毛利率 40%/43%，下修 3.5/4.7 百分點，EPS 為 4.63 元/9.03 元，年增
184%/95%，下修 31%/29%。因 InP 基板取得不確定性而下修目標本益比，
採估計 2027 年 EPS 以 45 倍目標本益比(前次採 57 倍)給予目標價 410 元，
目前評價合理給予持有之投資評等。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
InP 基板缺貨影響使 3Q26 獲利低於預期 23%至 EPS 1.26 元，且光通
訊占比為 25%，低於 1Q26/2Q26 的 45%/37%；4Q 估 EPS 1.31 元。 
⧫  
GaAs 應用之研發案增加，包含 VCSEL、QD Laser 及 PIN，有機會於
2028 年貢獻；此外，GaSb 於國防及商業需求均提升。 
⧫  
採日系基板為主且處於即到貨即用的緊俏狀態；2026 及 2027 年 EPS
估計下修 31%及 29%至 4.6 及 9.0 元，呈高度成長惟目前評價合理。

### Page 3

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體 2026 年 11 月 17 日 IET 第3 頁，共 14頁
上游 InP 基板緊缺難以完全解決，惟公司本身可望於 2H27 稍改善
IET-KY 目前光通訊出貨以 100G 及 200G 的 PD 為主，InP 基板短缺影響營運；因公司若干產品用
於美國軍工國防，故無採用上游中國大陸設廠之 AXT 的 InP 基板，目前採日系基板比重達 80%，
而且今年處於即到貨即用的緊俏狀態。德系供應商期待後續產能提升，且部份客戶自行提供基板；
然而整體基板於市場供需仍然緊俏，其他磊晶廠商亦積極搶占基板料源，基板供應商均已規劃大幅
度擴充產能惟仍需時間，估計二年內均呈緊缺狀況，公司則於 2H27 稍微改善。
GaAs(砷化鎵)應用之研發案增加，GaSb(銻化鎵)亦有新的商業需求
IET-KY 的 MBE 磊晶包含 GaAs(砷化鎵)、InP(磷化銦)、GaSb(銻化鎵)等材料，雖然 InP 現階段基
板不足，惟 GaAs 的研發案近期有所斬獲，如(1)射頻用於國防；(2)VCSEL，200G 射頻用於光通訊

(市場尚未有量產者)；(3)QD(量子點)雷射，發射端已於 2026 年中送樣再調整結構中；(4)PIN，用
於光通訊的接收端。此外，GaSb(銻化鎵)估計 2026 年增 65%以國防應用為主，近期亦有新的商業
需求正與客戶洽談中。
光聖換股已完成，3Q26 於業外挹注股利收入 0.16 億元
IET-KY 與光聖換股已於 5 月完成，透過股份交換結盟後，雙方相互持股比例分別為光聖持有 IET-
KY 約 15.26% 普通股股權，IET-KY 持有光聖約 1.79% 普通股股權。3Q26 於業外挹注股利收入
0.16 億元，短期尚未有其他本業的助益，長期有機會與其子公司合作開發產品。
MBE 磊晶在厚度控制及品質具優勢，估 2026/2027 年營運明顯年增但較先前保守
IET-KY 營運主體位於美國德州，以 MBE(Molecular Beam Epitaxy；分子束磊晶)技術為核心，同時
具備機器設備開發能力。MBE 磊晶在厚度控制及品質上準確度高於 MOCVD，適合新品研發及高階

產品。高階光模組應用於 2026 年以 400G 及 800G 為主流，多採用 100G 的 PD。現階段 IET 出貨
以 100G PD 及 200G PD(可用於 200G 光模組)為主。公司 MBE 機台為 16 台，估 2027 年增至 19
台。由於 InP 基板缺貨難解，本中心下修 2026/2027 年營收 13%/13%至 12.85 億/18.61 億元，年
增 19%/45%，毛利率 40%/43%，下修 3.5/4.7 百分點，EPS 為 4.63 元/9.03 元，年增 184%/95%，
下修 31%/29%。

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體 2026 年 11 月 17 日 IET 第4 頁，共 14頁  
圖 4：2025 年各季營收逐步走高，主要來自光通訊應用的貢獻

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧整理

圖 5：2025、2026F 及 2027F 年估計營收及營益率逐年走高

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧預估 
MBE 透過分子束提高均勻性、純度等，高階光通訊趨勢有利營運增長 
MBE (Molecular Beam Epitaxy)分子束磊晶製程為：(1)分子束形成：將坩鍋中的原料透過噴射源精
確加熱以昇華、蒸發分子形成分子束，分子如砷、鎵、磷、銦，噴嘴以石英克努森容器（Kundsen 
Cell）為主；(2)表面附著與擴散：分子束抵達加熱過的基板表面並吸附後將在晶格能量最低的地方
進行鍵結；(3)外延生長：透過精確控制基板溫度與分子束通量，薄膜會依照基板的晶體結構進行層
層堆疊最終形成磊晶。 
噴射源重要性在於透過熱管理、快門（Shutter）控制提供穩定、均勻的分子束，使薄膜品質穩定。
MBE 另外一大重要的設備為反射高能電子繞射儀 (Refection High-Energy Electron Diffraction, 
RHEED)，透過 RHEED 可以進行生長速率控制、生長狀態監控以即時控管磊晶品質。 
MBE 相較於 MOCVD 的優勢於：(1)精準度：透過分子束的控制更精準的製造且控制厚度；(2)減少
雜質：在原料端由於減去了前驅物的使用使材料純度更高，且基板遷移摻雜問題較小。然而，生長
速度較慢與且耗材要求較高，整體成本較高。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體 2026 年 11 月 17 日 IET 第5 頁，共 14頁  
產業概況  
光收發模組明顯成長且 800G 及 1.6T 零件更高階；NPO/CPO 數量低但高成長 
AI 對高速網路傳輸速率的需求持續攀升，光收發模組 (Optical Transceiver) 扮演關鍵角色；光收發
模組於 400G 以下且市場穩定；然而 400G 以上估年增 2026/2027 年為 80%/43%至 1 億/1.45 億個；
2026 年主流為 400G，2027 及 2028 年主流為 800G 且未來升級至 1.6T 以上則採更高階零組件，
如 Interposer、TEC 及 Submount 等電子級精密陶瓷。NPO/CPO 仍為初始階段，2026/2027/2028
年估 40 萬/320 萬/2,200 萬個：2027/2028 年增 680%/595%，惟數量遠低於光收發模組；CPO 將
OE 與交換機的 ASIC 共同封裝，降低傳輸功耗至 1/3；NPO 僅將 OE 與 ASIC 放置在交換機內同一
PCB 基板上，降低交換機傳輸功耗至 50%；二者均需插拔式 ELSFP 模組。

插拔式光收發模組以光晶片、PCB 及 DSP 最短缺 
插拔式光收發模組以光晶片、PCB 及 DSP 最短缺；內部架構以 DSP 為主晶片，其與 TIA 及 Driver
組成 EIC；PIC 則包含 PD、 LD、調變器； OE(光引擎)包含 EIC 與 PIC，所有零件在傳輸速率提升
下持續升級。PCB 在高階光模組需 mSAP 製程具門檻， DSP 由國際少數廠商寡占使供需相當緊俏，
內部的發射及接收端光元件晶粒以國際大廠為主。 
圖 7：光收發模組零件供應商分工架構(左)；光晶片上下游供應商以大廠為主(右)

資料來源：網路資料、元大投顧整理

圖 9：光收發模組中的 OE (左)；CPO/NPO 將 OE 置交換機內與 ASIC 共同封裝/同一 PCB 板 (右)

資料來源：市場資料、元大投顧整理

資料傳輸更高速使光晶片數量及規格提升；CPO/NPO 亦需插拔式光模組 
AI 交換機(Switch) 單埠（Port）的傳輸資料量主要取決於交換機的世代技術，2026 年主流為單 Port 
800G 的傳輸速率採 8 通道且單埠每秒可傳輸約 100 GB 的資料；1.6T（1.6 Tbps）採 8 通道且單
埠每秒可傳輸達 200 GB 的資料；未來若發展 3.2T 將採 8 通道且單埠每秒可傳輸達 400 GB 的資
料。 
此外，CPO（Co-Packaged Optics；共同封裝光學）光子積體電路（PIC）與電子積體電路（EIC）
共同封裝在同一個載板上，目前市場的 CPO/NPO 之 AI 交換機(Switch)，採 ELSFP（External 
Laser Small Form Pluggable，外接雷射光源模組），調變晶片靠近 ASIC 位置以縮短傳輸距離及降
低功耗；ELSFP 將容易受温度影響且壽命較短的雷射光源獨立出來，放在主機板邊緣作為標準化
插拔模組，不僅提升散熱效率，也解決 CPO/NPO 系統的維護難題。

### Page 7

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體 2026 年 11 月 17 日 IET 第7 頁，共 14頁
獲利調整與股票評價
MBE 磊晶法於高階應用具前景故提升機台產能，惟目前評價合理
IET-KY 為美國製造的 MBE(Molecular Beam Epitaxy；分子束磊晶)製程 III-V 族化合物磊晶廠，光
通訊(光纖網路)產品以應用於光收發模組為主要動能，占營收比重 2024 年的 29%升至 2025 年的
44%，帶動 2025 年毛利率年增 20 百分點至 36%。IET 的 100G 及 200G PD 可應用於 800G 及 1.6T
的光收發模組；未來市場朝 3.2T 邁進時需要 400G PD 且公司已進入研發階段，惟 2029 年後才有
顯著成長動能。
公司 MBE 機台為 16 台，估 2027 年增至 19 台。由於 InP 基板缺貨難解，本中心估計 2026 年光通
訊占比由 2025 年的 44%降至 35%，金額年減 5%；2027 年回到占比 44%，金額年增 79%。本中

心下修估計 2026/2027 年營收 13%/13%至 12.85 億/18.61 億元，年增 19%/45%，毛利率 40%/43%，
下修 3.5/4.7 百分點，EPS 為 4.63 元/9.03 元，年增 184%/95%，下修 31%/29%。因 InP 基板取得
不確定性而下修目標本益比，採估計 2027 年 EPS 以 45 倍目標本益比(前次採 57 倍)給予目標價
410 元，目前評價合理給予持有之投資評等。

### Page 11

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體 2026 年 11 月 17 日 IET 第11 頁，共 14頁

公司簡介  
生產基地位於美國的Ⅲ-Ⅴ族化合物半導體 MBE 磊晶製造商，光通訊比重最高且持續攀升 
IET-KY 成立於 2011 年，營運主體位於美國德州，為Ⅲ-Ⅴ族化合物半導體磊晶片代工廠，以 MBE 磊晶技術為核心，產品
包含 GaAs(砷化鎵)、InP(磷化銦)，GaSb(銻化鎵)；應用領域包含無線通訊、衛星通訊、光纖通訊、夜視鏡、保全監控、太
空、軍事、生物醫學、資料傳輸、雲端試算等，光通訊 PD 磊晶為近期主要成長動能。

1 20260327 526.00 650 650 買進 陳玫芬 
2 20260508 750.00 855 855 持有-超越同業 陳玫芬

資料來源：CMoney、元大投顧 
註：A = 未調整之收盤價；B = 未調整之目標價； C = 依據股本變動調整後之目標價。員工分紅稀釋影響未反映於 A、B 或 C。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

註：元大給予個股之目標價係依 12 個月投資期間計算。大中華探索系列報告並無正式之 12 個月目標價，其投資建議乃根據分析師報告中之指定期間分析
而得。

研 究 報 告                
旺矽(6223.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
探針卡需求強勁挹注短期營運動能強勁、CPO 設備進度如期  
 投資評價與建議 
目前探針卡廠商評價普遍修正至20~35倍，尤其以短期營運相對穩健之廠商
(如精測、旺矽)仍相對有撐，而我們觀察公司探針卡需求強勁支撐短期營運動
能強勁，並重申CPO設備驗證進度如期、客戶數持續增加，故仍看好評價仍有
望回到合理區間之中上緣。 
 重點評論分析 
4Q26營收有望雙位數季增：公司預估4Q26營收有望季增5~10%、毛利率
維持近3Q26之高檔水準，觀察探針卡BB Ratio均維持高檔水準，且擴產
計畫不變。基於公司10月營收達22.3億元創下新高、財測⼀向較為保守，
預估營收有望季增12.7%至66.6億元，毛利率61.8%，稅後EPS 21.82元。 
積極擴增MEMS產能、重申CPO客戶需求與進度如期：展望2027年：
(1)VPC和MEMS明年底有望擴至250萬針、600萬針，擴充幅度仍具有上
修空間，當前VPC主要客戶未有升級至MEMS規劃，至於MEMS主要貢獻
則來自於美系客戶伺服器CPU；(2)insertion 2客戶將於12月通知驗證結
果，1H27有望獲得量產機訂單，至於insertion 3已如期出貨工程機給3間
客戶，並預期1H27將陸續獲得量產機訂單，惟公司預估CPO設備營收顯
著貢獻時間點將落在2028年，故我們同步調整營收、獲利。 
 投資風險 
CPO進展不如預期、探針卡擴產針數下修。

| 營運概況 
3Q26獲利優於預期：3Q26營收季增12.8%至59億元，連續七季創下歷史新高，毛利率61.7%，稅後EPS 19.18元，
其中營收、獲利均優於預期，推估主因為探針卡產品組合改善，加上於2Q26進行之探針卡報價調整完整貢獻3Q
營收(包含CPC漲價20%、取消VPC和MEMS 10%價格優惠)，另公司營業費用管控得宜也同步挹注獲利。 
估4Q26營收有望雙位數季增：公司預估4Q26營收有望季增5~10%、毛利率有望維持近3Q26之高檔水準，我們
推估動能包含：(1)維持VPC、MEMS月產能今年底分別擴至200萬針、350萬針之進展；(2)訂單出貨則觀察探針卡
BB Ratio均維持高檔水準，其中CPC今年前10個月維持滿載、目前BB Ratio接近1，至於MEMS、VPC則超過1，
考量公司探針卡交期已明顯延⻑至4~6個月，隱含本季探針卡營收仍有望持續提升。基於公司10月營收達22.3億
元創下新高、財測⼀向較為保守，預估本季營收有望季增12.7%至66.6億元，毛利率61.8%，稅後EPS 21.82元。 
積極擴增MEMS產能、重申CPO客戶需求與進度如期：展望2027年，我們觀察：(1)探針卡中，明年CPC月產能維
持50萬針、VPC和MEMS明年底則有望擴至250萬針、600萬針，產能擴充幅度具有上修空間，且強調2028年維持
積極擴產策略，訂單貢獻中，VPC主要客戶近年未有升級至MEMS探針卡之規劃，預期該客戶AI ASIC訂單將支撐
明年VPC需求，至於MEMS明年主要貢獻則來自於美系客戶伺服器CPU，且公司持續爭取潛在客戶AI ASIC訂單，
另規劃於2H27量產之PCB廠有望挹注探針卡成本優化；(2)CPO設備中，insertion 2如我們先前所提，於3Q26底
～4Q26初送樣至客戶端進行驗證，公司預估客戶將於12月通知驗證結果，根據目前客戶回饋正向，預期1H27有
望獲得量產機訂單，此外近期有另⼀客戶與公司接洽中(公司預估1H27獲得驗證結果)，至於insertion 3則已如期
出貨工程機給3間客戶，也將陸續交機給另3間客戶，預計1H27將陸續獲得insertion 3量產機訂單，惟考量接單至
出貨、裝機驗收之時間較⻑，公司認為CPO相關設備顯著營收貢獻時間點將落在2028年，我們則同步下修明年相
關設備之營收認列數量。綜上所述，公司預估明年營收有望年增至少50%、毛利率維持在60%以上；我們則預估
明年營收有望年增58.6%至344.6億元，較先前下修主要反映部分CPO設備遞延至2028年認列營收，毛利率63.2%，
稅後EPS 117.77元。

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 500,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
231,000 
반도체업종

◼ 3분기 실적발표에 대한 시장의 반응은 다소 냉소적. 
◼ 주주환원은 시점의 문제일 뿐. 강화 방향성은 여전히 유효. 
◼ 메모리반도체 이익의 폭증이 가격의 매력을 유발. 비중확대 전략 유효.

매력적인 가격, 변하지 않은 주주환원 정책의 방향성 
목표주가와 매수의견 유지. 메모리반도체 주도 폭발적 이익 상승이 가격 (주
가)의 매력을 지속 유발하고 있고, 주주환원 정책의 강화 방향성에도 변한 것
은 없다는 판단. 절대적으로 싼 주가를 비중 확대의 기회로 삼을 필요. 
1. 주주환원, 시점의 문제 
금번 실적발표에 대한 시장의 반응은 다소 냉소적. 대외 불확실성이 큰 만큼, 
시장은 자사주 매입/소각과 같은 즉각적 주가 부양 정책의 발표를 기대.  
당사는 삼성전자의 주주환원이 “시점의 문제”라 생각. 총 주주환원의 규모도, 
차기 (2027-2029년) 주주환원 정책의 강화 방향에도 변한 것은 없다는 판
단. 시장이 기대하는 자사주 매입/소각은 우선주 중심으로 시행될 가능성이 
높으며, 시행 시, 그 규모는 25조원 내외일 것 (DPS 12,567원)으로 전망. 
2. 메모리반도체, 더 올라갈 것 
보수적 환율 전망 (2027년 평균 환율 1,300원 적용)에도, 2027년 메모리반도
체 영업이익은 608조원에 달할 것. 이익 전망의 상향 여력 또한 유효. 
1) 최대 생산, 최대 판매 
삼성전자는 금일 4Q26 DRAM 판매량 가이던스로 전분기 대비 10% 중후반 
성장 (q-q +18%로 추정)을 제시. 일부 투자자들은 높아진 기저 부담으로 
2027년 판매 성장이 제한될 것을 우려. 당사는 높아진 기저에도 2027년 
DRAM 판매가 전년대비 20% 상승할 것으로 전망. 
- 극단적 수급 불균형 속, 삼성전자는 “최대 생산, 최대 판매” 정책을 시행 
중. 삼성전자만의 강력한 Execution이 20% 대 성장을 유지시킬 원동력. 
- DRAM 1c 수율의 조기 개선 속, 범용 DRAM 양산에 DRAM 1c Capa가 일
부 투입될 것으로 예상. Bit 성장을 높일 수 있는 변화가 될 것. 
2) 열려 있는 가격의 상방 
1Q27 모바일 DRAM에서 Surprise가 발생할 것이라는 의견을 유지 (전분기 
대비 30% 이상 상승). 장기계약이 체결된 CSP향으로도 가격의 추가 상승 여
력이 존재한다는 점을 상기할 필요. CSP의 경우, 당장의 비용 부담보다 AI 시
장 선점을 우선시. 장기계약으로 형성된 가격 Band의 천장 (Ceiling) 또한 시
장 우려 대비 낮지 않음을 감안할 필요. 2027년 범용 DRAM ASP는 전년대비 
40% 중후반까지 상승할 가능성 (현 전망: 전년대비 34% 상승).  
3) HBM, 제품 경쟁력과 점유율의 동반 성장  
최근 삼성전자향으로 HBM4/HBM4e 추가 공급 요청이 쇄도하고 있는 것으
로 추정. 일부 고객은 연내 추가 공급 시 2027년 계약 가격을 선제 지급할 
의사를 표출. 제품 경쟁력이 회복되었음을 보여주는 증거라 생각. 
제품 경쟁력의 회복과 최대 Capa의 이점 속, 2027년은 HBM 점유율 1위를 
확보하는 기념비적 해가 될 것으로 전망 (매출액 기준 점유율 38% 예상).

KOSPI 5937.66

Report source: Hankyung

Status: ok

### Page 1

(329180) 
HD 현대중공업 
2026.10.29

AI 로보틱스/방산/조선/운송 양승윤 
6139 / syyang0901@eugenefn.com

피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 610,000 원(하향) 
 현재주가 285,500 원(10/28)

 3Q26 매출액은 5조 7,536억원(+30.2%yoy), 영업이익은 8,992억원
(+61.3%yoy, OPM 15.6%)을 기록. 영업이익 컨센서스 9,792억원 하회.  
 평균 환율이 약 79원 하락하며 영업이익 약 450억원이 줄었고, 조업
일수는 47일로 통상 계절성 대비 4일 추가 감소해 약 500억원의 이
익 감소 효과 발생. 임단협 추가 비용의 약 75%도 3분기에 선반영
(잔여분은 4분기 반영). 일회성 요인을 제외하면 이익 체력은 견조. 
 동사의 조선 부문 매출액은 4조 3,988억원(+44.0%yoy), 영업이익은 
6,463억원(+62.8%yoy, OPM 14.7%)을 기록. 상선 OPM은 14.7%로 
2Q26(17.8%) 대비 하락했으나 대부분 외생변수 영향. 3Q26 대형선 매출 
중 2023년 수주분 비중은 45%, 2024~2025년 수주분은 51%. 고선가 
물량의 매출 비중이 높아지는 2027년까지 수익성 개선 여력은 충분.  
 9월 말 누적 상선 수주는 138.0억달러로 연간 목표의 120%, 엔진기계는 
41.4억달러로 155%를 달성했으나 함정(13%)과 해양(5%)은 부진. LNG선
은 중국 조선소의 저가 수주와 발주 둔화로 선가가 일부 하락. 선주들이 현 
선가를 저점으로 인식하며 발주 협의를 재개하는 분위기. 북미 LNG 프로젝
트 FID가 이어지고 있어 2027년 LNG선 발주 회복 기대. 
 함정 부문은 매출 2,570억원(-14.6%qoq)으로 줄었으나, 수출 비중 확대에 
따른 믹스 개선으로 영업이익 386억원(+114%qoq, OPM 15.0%)을 기록. 
해양플랜트는 매출액 3,799억원(+35.3%yoy), 영업이익 1,265억원(OPM 
33.3%). C/O 180억원을 제외해도 Ruya 공정 본격화와 Trion 고수익 물량
으로 높은 수익성 유지. 2027년 일부 프로젝트 인도로 매출 감소가 예상되
나, LNG선·블록 물량 투입으로 고정비 부담을 완화할 계획. 신규 해상변전
소(OSS) 계약을 앞두고 있으며, 2027년 초 추가 수주 발표도 기대. 
 주목할 부분은 엔진기계. 영업이익 2,299억원(OPM 24.2%)으로 높은 D/F 
엔진 비중을 바탕으로 견조한 수익성 유지. 육상발전용 힘센엔진은 올해   
3GW를 확보하며 2029년까지 계획 캐파 수준의 물량을 이미 채움. 생산 
예상 물량은 2028년 약 1.5GW, 2029년 약 3GW이며, 신규 공장 구축 이
후 연간 생산능력은 약 4GW로 확대 예정. 북미 Hyperscaler와의 직접 협
상이 늘고 있으며, 힘센엔진을 활용한 부유식 발전설비(BMPP)로 FDC 시장
까지 대응 가능. AI 데이터센터 전력 수요가 2028년 이후 새로운 이익 축. 
 미국에서는 Kiewit과 합작법인 설립 계약 체결. 군수지원함 등 지원함 사업
부터 추진하며, 현지 조선소 건설 과정에서 HII에 선박 블록을 공급하는 방
안도 협의 중. 구체적인 투자계획은 2026년 말까지 확정 목표. 국내에서는 
핵추진잠수함 초기 예산이 2027년 반영되며 동사도 사업 참여를 준비 중. 
 동사에 대한 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 61만원을 제시. 고선가 물량 매출 확
대와 육상발전용 엔진 성장으로 피크아웃을 이겨낼 굴지의 조선사.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

### Page 1

他在博客中透露，他⼏乎可以确信AI实验室已取得尚未公开的重⼤密码学突破，引发外界
对RSA和椭圆曲线加密安全性的担忧。与此同时，⾕歌AI已在整数分解等领域取得进展，
英国央⾏也警⽰前沿AI带来的⽹络安全⻛险，敦促⾦融机构加快密码技术升级。
摘要：
AI可能已经找到了破解现有密码系统的⽅法，只是还没公布。
不er，OpenAI和A社到底还藏了多少东西？？
前脚两家才双双宣布证明了P vs NP的代数版本VP≠VNP。
后脚，理论计算机科学界⼤佬Scott Aaronson就爆出了⼀个更惊⼈的消息：
（标题也是相当直⽩了，HOLY SHITx4）
u1s1，要是别⼈说这话我可能还有所怀疑，但Scott就不⼀样了。
这位是得克萨斯⼤学奥斯汀分校计算机科学教授，量⼦计算和计算复杂性理论领域的知名⼤
佬。
⽽且，他还有⼀重相当微妙的⾝份——OpenAI前研究员，曾在OpenAI专⻔研究AI安全问题。
专业对⼝，⼜在AI⼤⼚待过，能⽐咱们提前吃到点⽠，倒也不奇怪。（doge）
也难怪Scott的爆料⼀出，数学圈、AI圈、密码学圈的⼈瞬间坐不住了。。。

我从多个可靠消息源获悉，⼏乎可以确信，AI实验室⼿⾥还握着⼀枚甚⾄多枚密码学「重磅炸
弹」。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⼤家猜来猜去，最终将⽬光锁定在了⼀个关键问题上：
⽀撑RSA加密体系的⼤整数分解难题，该不会已经被AI拿下了吧？？
要知道这事⼉⼀旦成真，影响的可就不只是数学界了。
咱们平时⽹购付款、登录⽹站、传输机密⽂件，背后都有密码技术在保驾护航，⽽RSA正是其
中⼴泛使⽤的公钥密码算法之⼀。
兹事体⼤，连《Nature》都闻着味⼉来了，专⻔发⽂追问：
AI已经破解互联⽹加密了吗？
OpenAI和A社，⼿⾥可能还有⼤招
这事⼉还得从Scott的⼀顿晚饭说起。
就在OpenAI和A社双双宣布证明VP≠VNP的当天，他正和⼏位朋友、同事⼀起聚餐。
结果⼀顿饭的⼯夫，OpenAI⼜接连放出了矩阵乘法、快速傅⾥叶变换相关的数学研究成果。
这速度，直接给⼀桌⼦研究理论计算机科学的⼈整不会了。
⼤家⼀边吃饭，⼀边拿着⼿机和电脑，疯狂刷新OpenAI和A社的GitHub仓库。
毕竟现在这两家连新闻稿都懒得发了，谁知道下⼀次刷新，⼜有哪个困扰⼈类⼏⼗年的数学难
题被解决了。。。

### Page 3

⽽且疯狂刷新之外，曾在OpenAI待过的Scott不免担⼼——
放出来的成果都已经这么惊⼈了，那些还没放出来的呢？
事实上，早在上⼀篇博客的评论区，就有⽹友向他打听过：
OpenAI或者Anthropic，是不是已经找到了整数分解的多项式时间算法（Factoring in P）？
彼时，Scott就透露，⾃⼰已经听到了不少传闻，称OpenAI和A社⾄少有⼀家已经找到了这样的
算法。
不过，眼看这事⼉越传越⼴，Scott后来⼜给这条回复加上了删除线，提醒⼤家不要把传闻当成
实锤。
⽽到了最新这篇博客⾥，Scott⼲脆把⾃⼰能够确认到什么程度，给说清楚了：
I’ve now heard many, many rumors that one or both labs are sitting on Factoring in P.
我⼏乎可以确信，AI实验室⼿⾥还握着尚未公开的重⼤密码学突破。⾄于具体是不是整数分解，
⽬前还不好说。

### Page 4

不er，为啥⼤家都盯上了整数分解啊？
咱们来做⼀道数学题。
17×23等于多少？
391，虽然已经过了智商巅峰期，但花点时间⼝算⼀下咱还是能搞定。
但如果反过来，只告诉你391，让你找出它是哪两个质数相乘得到的呢？
显然就要多费点⼯夫了。
再把这两个质数换成各有⼏百位的数字，正着乘，对计算机来说依旧轻轻松松，反过来分解难
度却会急剧飙升。
RSA加密算法的重要安全基础，恰恰就是这种计算难度上的不对称。
过去⼏⼗年，数学家们⼀直在尝试寻找更⾼效的整数分解算法。
如果AI真的找到了⾜够实⽤的多项式时间算法，那么RSA的安全基础也就被动摇了。
⽽且，还真别说，已经有AI开始在这个⽅向上交作业了。
就在11⽉25⽇，⾕歌⼀⼝⽓公开了6篇由AI系统Cogentic⽣成的数学论⽂。

### Page 5

仔细⼀看，其中有两篇研究的正是整数分解，还有⼀篇涉及密码学中的另⼀个重要数学难题
——离散对数。
虽然⾕歌⽬前公开的整数分解算法还没有达到多项式时间，但⾄少从这些论⽂来看，AI已经开始
在密码学所依赖的数学难题上取得进展了。
然后，问题就来了——
⾕歌已经在这个⽅向上交了作业，那最近动不动就给数学界整出⼀场「地震」的OA两家呢？
再结合Scott的爆料……
嘶，不敢细想。。。
甚⾄有reddit⽹友已经发现了⼀个耐⼈寻味的细节：
就，真的很不对劲。
以前抢着发论⽂，现在可能不敢发了？
反正坐不住，那不如反向「试探」OpenAI⼀把？？
此前OpenAI⼀⼝⽓公布了涉及722道数学问题的研究成果，⼏乎涵盖数学的各个领域，唯独不
⻅密码学的踪影。

### Page 6

就在今天，X上有位叫Cassie Heart的⽹友爆料，⾃⼰在研究ECC（椭圆曲线密码学）时，遇到
了⼀件怪事。
同样的研究思路，之前GPT Astra 6还愿意陪⾃⼰往下研究，换成Astra 6.2 ，诶，直接不让聊
了。
这位⽹友当即就警觉起来：
要知道，ECC和前⾯提到的RSA⼀样，也是当今主流的公钥密码技术，⼴泛应⽤于HTTPS安全
通信、数字签名等场景。
为了防⽌哪天OpenAI突然甩出⼀篇论⽂，把功劳全揽⾛了，这位⽹友索性把⾃⼰研究思路对应
的SHA-256哈希值给Po到了⽹上，提前留个证。
当然，光凭模型拒绝继续讨论，还不能说明OpenAI真的取得了突破。
但这事⼉想想也挺微妙的。
前⼏天，OA两家为了争夺VP≠VNP的⾸发权，恨不得把做出证明的时间精确到⼏点⼏分。
可如果真有⼀家率先攻破了RSA或者ECC……
这第⼀，还敢抢吗？
毕竟，按照斯坦福⼤学知名密码学家Dan Boneh的说法，⼀旦AI找到了破解主流公钥密码系统
的新⽅法，整个互联⽹都可能受到波及。
更关键的是，这事⼉甚⾄都不⽤等到P vs NP问题被解决。

AI Weekly在报道此事时就特意指出，RSA和ECC所依赖的数学难题，⾄今都没有被证明是NP
完全问题。
就是说，AI甚⾄都不⽤拿下P vs NP这个世纪难题，就有可能攻破现有的密码系统。
OpenAI该不会已经快把ECC给破了吧？？

### Page 7

想到这⼉，再回头看看最近数学圈的画⻛，就有点魔幻了。
随着AI接连拿下数学难题，⽹上也掀起了⼀股给AI「找茬」的热潮。
有⼈质疑证明的正确性，有⼈质疑AI究竟有没有原创能⼒，还有⼈⼲脆让AI去反驳AI给出的证
明。
⼀时间，数学证明和针对证明的「反驳」满天⻜。
对此，Scott也在博客⾥引⽤了⾃⼰⼀位朋友的评论，表达了态度：
Anyway，这事⼉短时间内也很难吵出个结果。
但考虑到AI可能带来的⻛险，与密码学深度绑定的⾦融圈已经开始⾏动了。
就在当地时间11⽉26⽇，英国央⾏发布了最新⼀期⾦融稳定报告。
报告专⻔提到了前沿AI可能带来的⽹络安全⻛险，同时还在催促⾦融机构加快向抗量⼦密码技术
迁移。
经过多年来那些声称千禧年难题已被解决的荒谬「解答」之后，现在当我们有了真正的解答时，
却⼜出现了荒谬的「反驳」，声称千禧年难题并未被解决。从某种意义上说，这简直是个绝妙的
讽刺。

### Page 8

写评论
说起来，过去这么多年，⼈类⼀直在为量⼦计算机可能带来的密码学威胁做准备。
新的密码算法都研究出来了，各国也陆陆续续开始部署了。
谁承想呢？
量⼦计算机还没等来，隔壁天天做数学题的AI倒是可能先来抢活了。。。
⽽且，抗量⼦密码算法也有⾃⼰的数学安全假设，能防住量⼦计算机的已知攻击算法，可不意
味着就能防住AI找到的全新解法。
最扎⼼的是，照现在这个势头，AI在数学上取得⼀次重⼤突破，可能就是Scott吃顿晚饭的⼯
夫。
但全球银⾏、企业、政府机构要想把⾃家的密码系统全换⼀遍……
那可不是多吃⼏顿饭就能解决的事⼉了。
One More Thing
在⻅证近期⼀系列AI数学突破后，Scott第⼀次真切地感受到，「P≠NP的证明可能已经不远了」。
他甚⾄感慨，过去这⼀周发⽣的事情，和⾃⼰想象中AI奇点到来的样⼦⾮常相似。

只希望这场奇点，仅仅局限在他们那个研究数学难题的⼩⼩世界⾥。
但现在看来，好像已经有点难了。。。

Document 296: Central banks_ a pause while waiting for more information (2026-10-29)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Central banks_ a pause while waiting for more information (2026-10-29).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 30 October 2026

EcoFlash

Central banks: a pause while waiting for more information

As expected, the Fed, the ECB, and the BoJ left their rates unchanged this week. This pause, however, does not mark 
the end of the cycle. The ECB’s tone is becoming more balanced, weighing inflationary risks against tightening financial 
conditions, but one final rate hike in December remains our base case. Across the Atlantic, Kevin Warsh describes an 
environment conducive to further tightening, while remaining cautious about the risk of a credit shock. In Japan, the 
lasting exit from the deflationary cycle is taking shape, and monetary policy adjustment is expected to continue 
gradually. We anticipate two rate hikes by the Fed by January and four by the BoJ by the second half of 2028.

Eurozone: A more balanced tone, but the course remains unchanged. The ECB’s statement marks a shift from September. 
It now explicitly acknowledges both upside risks to inflation (the magnitude and persistence of the energy shock) and downside 
pressures (tightening financial conditions). The statement that inflation would remain “well above target for an extended period” 
no longer appears. In essence, however, there is little change. A rate hike in December remains our base case scenario, widely 
anticipated and implicitly signaled by Christine Lagarde during the Q&A (“If the current situation is not called into question by the 
next set of economic data, the path is clear”). Lagarde presented the rise in sovereign yields as a global phenomenon that is 
tightening financial conditions; regarding the TPI, she stuck to the usual stance: the ECB will intervene if monetary policy 
transmission were to be hampered “by disorderly movements not justified by fundamentals”. By December, the indirect and 
second-round effects of the energy shock will be closely scrutinised. From our perspective, September’s producer prices offer 
clearer signals, but the increase remains limited to a few sectors: energy, electricity-intensive industries and electrical equipment, 
driven by IA1. For now, companies in the Eurozone, particularly in the services sector, appear to be absorbing the shock. The 
European Commission’s survey released this Thursday indicates a rise in the sales price index in October, which remains in line 
with recent months.

United States: The Fed navigates between risks. The FOMC pauses after September’s rate hike and maintains the federal 
funds target at 3.75%-4.0%. However, Fed Chairman Kevin Warsh outlined a landscape conducive to further tightening: stronger 
growth, “rather positive” labor market conditions, and, above all, inflation that is “too high.” As a result, two dissenting votes (out 
of 12) were cast in favor of a 25bps hike. But the committee also appears to fear the adverse consequences of tightening too 
quickly, which is why it is waiting to “accumulate more information.” AI is increasingly at the heart of monetary policymaking. In 
particular, K. Warsh warned against the risks of an abrupt tightening of interest rate conditions, including for the AI investment 
cycle - which triggered a steepening of the yield curve. He also announced that he would soon (on November 16) attend a hearing 
of the “Productivity and Employment” task force to “refine his views” on the implications of AI for the dual mandate. Governor Lisa 
Cook, who voted for a 25bps hike, had noted ahead of the committee meeting “ongoing inflationary pressures” and a risk that they 
might not “subside quickly”. In addition, K. Warsh announced that there would be fewer meetings resulting in rate decisions: 7 in 
2027 and 6 in 2028, compared with 8 today. The change is expected to take effect starting in the fall of 2027. The meetings freed 
up by this change would allow the committee to focus on “strategic deliberations” and long-term issues.

Japan: A status quo, but a tightening cycle set to continue as the economy emerges from deflation. The Bank of Japan 
decided, in line with our forecasts and the consensus, to keep its policy rate target steady at 1.25% at its October 29-30 meeting. 
One dissenting vote called for a rate hike (25bps), contrasting with the two dovish dissenting votes (opposed to a rate hike) at the 
previous meeting. As in the US, the decision is justified by the need to further analyse risks to economic activity, prices, and 
financial conditions. Above all, the central bank’s outlook for economic activity and prices marks a turning point: it explicitly states 
that “core inflation has reached a level generally in line with price stability” and that it is “expected to remain there.” Governor Ueda 
refined this by noting that it is now necessary to “anchor” inflation at this level before declaring the target achieved. Furthermore, 
the emphasis on “upside risks to prices” and the fact that any rise in core inflation is now deemed “undesirable” indicate that 
monetary tightening will continue.

The monetary tightening cycle continues. The next steps are likely to be more significant in the United States, where inflation 
has exceeded the target for nearly six years and coexists with a favorable outlook for growth and employment. In the Eurozone, 
the rise in inflation is primarily due to a supply shock in energy costs (though it is also fueled by demand in the United States). It 
continues to have little impact on measures of core inflation, including those tracked by the ECB, such as the HICP; and 
households’ long-term inflation expectations remain anchored. The degree of monetary tightening also differs. In real terms, the 
ECB’s current rates (the deposit facility rate deflated by the core CPI) hover around 0%, a level that more or less corresponds to 
estimates of the natural rate. The Fed, meanwhile, remains in accommodative territory: the real rate (Fed Funds deflated by Core

1 This is a point underscored by the first PPI releases for September (Spain, Italy).

### Page 2

PCE) is at around 0.8%, 40bps below the committee’s implied long-term level (1.2%). Japan remains an outlier: the rise in rates 
preceded the inflationary shock and, until now, has been more a matter of normalization. It is therefore more extensive and spread 
out over time. The central bank is adjusting to the new inflation regime and remains cautious in its exit from accommodative 
policies. The starting point is significantly lower, with real rates still negative, but the adjustment cycle should ultimately bring it to 
the upper end of the neutral range estimated by the BoJ (1.1%-2.5%). We therefore anticipate one final rate hike by the ECB in 
December, two more hikes by the Fed, first in December and then in January, and four spaced-out hikes by the BoJ through the 
second half of 2028.

Anis Bensaidani   
Economist, United States, Japan 
anis.bensaidani@bnpparibas.com

Guillaume Derrien 
Economist, Europe

Document 301: Cliff Notes_ risks mount (2026-11-27)

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: risks mount
By Ryan Wells, Elliot Clarke & Illiana Jain
10:31 November 20 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
Australia’s labour market disappointed again in October, employment falling 43.5k following
September’s 36.4k decline. On a three-month average basis, job gains over the year have now slowed
to 1.1%yr, materially below current growth in the working-age population, 1.7%yr. As a result, the

unemployment rate rose to a new post-pandemic high of 4.9% despite the participation rate falling
another 0.2ppts to 66.6%.

### Page 2

While employment growth’s descent initially reflected softer momentum in hiring, now we are seeing
evidence of a material increase in redundancies. Most affected workers look to have remained in
employment to date, but persistent job shedding raises the risk of a faster and more persistent uptrend
in the unemployment rate, potentially above our baseline peak forecast of 5.2% (at mid-2027) and most
certainly before the late-2028 peak of 5.1% the RBA expects.

As such, the robust 1.1% (3.5%yr) rise in the Q3 Wage Price Index warrants close scrutiny. The Q3
increase was skewed towards those on awards and enterprise arrangements – pay outcomes directly
tied to the Fair Work Commission’s decision and collective bargaining. Individual arrangements
meanwhile, the most responsive to current and expected labour market conditions, accounted for their
smallest share of a Q3 wage increase since 2019. Business survey detail also suggests labour cost

growth will normalise quickly after the award increase.
Deteriorating economic conditions are also increasingly evident in housing. The Westpac Housing
Sentiment Index fell 2.2% to 83.2 in November, among the weakest readings since 2005. Interestingly,
there remains considerable latent demand: almost 13% of consumers intend to purchase a home over
the next year, and relatively few plan to sell. For now, though, weak sentiment, high interest rates and

expectations of further price declines are keeping would-be buyers on the sidelines. These factors also
pose risks to the broader growth story.
In the US, abstracting from the Anthropic IPO, the outlook for inflation remained the critical theme. The
October FOMC minutes highlighted that inflation is currently being supported by tariffs and energy
prices as well as the AI buildout, with "several [members] judg[ing] that price pressures were
increasingly extending beyond industries directly affected by data center construction”. Participants

"assessed that medium- and longer-term inflation expectations continued to be consistent with the
Committee's 2 percent objective", although persistent above-target inflation is a threat to the status
quo, and perceived risks are skewed to the upside. FOMC members who spoke throughout the week
also kept the focus on price risks, and called for the FOMC to act swiftly. Governor Waller again
proposed a 50bp hike in December; others seemingly favour a 25bp hike at the December and January

meetings, as Westpac forecasts, and continuing to tighten further in 2027 if necessary.
Very clearly, the labour market and activity growth are not of concern for US policy makers. Retail sales
gave further justification for this view this week, the control group surprising to the upside with a 0.6%
gain in October after a material upward revision to September (0.5%). That said, housing is under
considerable pressure, pending home sales falling 17.1% over the past year, and housing starts back

at 2019 levels despite a 9.9% rebound in October. Persistent weakness in this sector will, in time, have
implications for both consumption and investment.
China’s October data round was the other key international development of the week. Again, it provided
a call for pro-active stimulus in scale. In the month, annual retail sales growth almost stalled at 0.4%yr
and the decline in fixed asset investment broadened outside property, with capital expenditure now

down 8.1% across the economy year-to-date. Industrial production again highlighted that its output to
satiate global demand which is holding up aggregate growth; though, even here, momentum softened
in October, annual growth in production slowing from 5.5%yr to 4.8%yr. It is not only activity that

### Page 3

Related articles
remains under pressure, but also wealth, with new / existing home prices 0.15% / 0.24% lower in the
month. As we continue to emphasise, to prove effective, stimulus needs to affect change in both activity
and wealth and do so in a sustainable manner.

Cliff Notes: cost of living front and centre
November 13 2026 • 5 mins

Document 302: Construction rotates from infrastructure to housing and data centres (2026-11-27)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Construction rotates from infrastructure to housing and data centres (2026-11-27).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-27
Pages: 18
Status: ok

### Page 1

#australia #construction
Construction rotates from infrastructure to housing and data
centres
By Pat Bustamante
Senior Economist, Westpac
14:36 November 25 2026 Share
Construction activity rose 0.9%qtr in Q3 and 5.8%yr. Construction activity remained solid,
driven by residential building (+3.1%qtr), non-residential building (+1.5%qtr, includes data
centres), partly offset by engineering construction (-0.9%qtr).

### Page 2

Construction activity rose 0.9%qtr in Q3 2026, taking annual growth to a strong 5.8%. The quarterly
increase was close to Westpac Economics’ 0.8%qtr forecast, but well above the 0.4%qtr market
consensus. Importantly, sizeable upward revisions to private construction lifted annual growth by
almost 2ppts. These revisions suggest the economy was somewhat stronger in 2025–26 than
previously thought, despite the same observed inflation outcomes.

What does this mean for the near-term outlook?
Overall, today’s data show construction activity continuing to expand at a solid pace, although there are
important offsetting forces beneath the headline result. Public infrastructure work remains soft and is
expected to ease further as major east-coast projects reach completion. This is being partly offset by
public non-residential construction, particularly health, schools and other social infrastructure.
At the same time, private construction remains strong. Residential construction is up 9.3% over the
year, the commercial building pipeline, which includes data centres, is around record highs, and the
renewable-energy investment pipeline remains substantial as we work towards the 82% renewable
electricity target by 2030.
Taken together, demand in the construction sector is likely to remain elevated in the near term, but its

### Page 3

composition is shifting. The pullback in public infrastructure is freeing up labour and other construction
capacity for housing, data centres, renewables and commercial projects. This rebalancing has
already been substantial, with our estimates suggesting the government footprint in the
construction industry declined by around 1.5ppts in 2025–26 (Government’s footprint plateaus as
services rise and construction retreats).
What does this mean for the National Accounts?

Today’s release is the first of a set of partials which will feed into next week’s September quarter 2026
National Accounts. Today’s numbers alone point to stronger growth in residential and new buildings
activity over the quarter and some upside for our Q3 estimate. However, with several important partial
indicators still to come, we will provide our full assessment in Friday’s GDP preview.
What about inflationary pressures and the impact of the data centre boom?

The spike in construction costs during the pandemic was largely synchronised across dwellings, non-
residential buildings and engineering construction. Today, however, cost pressures differ as demand
profiles across these segments diverge.
While rebounding in the quarter, engineering construction costs have been softer as major public
infrastructure programs wind down. In contrast, dwelling construction costs are accelerating as activity
in this segment picks up. Non-residential building costs appear to be going sideways, rather than

significantly reaccelerating, despite the strength in private investment, including data centres.

### Page 4

At 4.5%yr, aggregate construction cost growth is above what was recorded over 2018-19. This raises
questions about whether the strong demand in pockets like data centre investment are contributing to
broader inflationary pressures. For now, there is limited evidence of this happening. However,
construction cost deflators continue to firm in year-ended terms, suggesting that the disinflation
anticipated by the RBA may take longer to materialise.
How this plays out going forward depends on how much the substantial roll-off in public construction

activity makes room for the pickup in private residential and non-residential building work, particularly
data centres, and in private engineering construction associated with the renewable energy transition.
Total construction work yet to be done has risen to around 9.2% of nominal GDP in Q2 2026, compared
with an average of around 8.8% since 2022.
If that pipeline continues to build faster than public infrastructure releases capacity,
construction-sector cost pressures could prove more persistent than currently anticipated.

However, this is not our central case, as higher interest rates and ongoing cost increases are
likely to dampen private demand, while the public infrastructure pipeline continues to wind
down.

### Page 5

The detail
Residential construction outperformed, rising 3.1%qtr in Q3, following an upwardly revised 2.8%qtr
increase in Q2 2026 (previously estimated at 1.7%qtr). Growth was driven by a 3.9%qtr increase in
private residential construction, its strongest quarterly gain since June 2005, outside the COVID period.
In year-ended terms, residential construction is up 9.3%yr, the strongest growth rate since the
September quarter of 2016. This suggests the sector is responding to growing housing demand by

expanding output despite ongoing cost pressures. Some of this strength may also reflect the pullback
in public construction activity, which is freeing up labour and other resources for residential and non-
residential projects.
The strength in residential construction suggests the sector is responding more meaningfully to
Australia's housing shortage. However, with approvals likely to soften as higher interest rates bite and
demand moderates, the current pace of expansion may prove difficult to sustain.

### Page 6

Engineering construction (EC) declined 0.9%qtr in Q3 2026 but was 1.3%higher in year-ended
terms.
As foreshadowed in this month’s Market Outlook, the public infrastructure pipeline appears to have
passed its peak, as major transport projects across Australia’s east coast, particularly in NSW and
Victoria, move towards completion. Consistent with this, public engineering construction declined
3.0%year in Q3 but stabilised over the quarter to post a gain of 2.9%qtr. More broadly, the trend

remains weak, with activity falling in five of the past seven quarters.

### Page 7

In contrast, private engineering construction remains robust, up almost 5% over the year. However,
activity fell 3.7%qtr in Q3, suggesting some moderation may also be emerging in this segment. One
area of continued strength is electricity infrastructure, with the value of private work on electricity
generation and distribution rising by an estimated 10%qtr in seasonally adjusted and nominal terms,
largely reflecting renewable energy projects such as wind and solar developments.

Looking ahead, the private engineering pipeline remains supported by structural investment themes,
particularly the energy transition and nationwide rollout of data centres. However, constraints around
utility provision, grid connections and supporting infrastructure remain a key risk to the timely delivery
of these projects.
Non-residential building construction increased 1.5%qtr in Q3 2026, leaving activity 10.2% higher
over the year. Private-sector activity was mixed, declining 0.4%qtr, but remained 11% higher in year-

ended terms. Public activity also picked up sharply, rising 6.2%qtr to be 8.3% higher over the year,
partly reflecting investment in health and other facilities required to service a growing population.
Overall, non-residential construction remains strong, supported by two key investment themes: the
rollout of data centres in the private sector and increased public investment in health and other social
infrastructure, including in Queensland.

### Page 8

Although the contribution from individual projects is difficult to quantify, the surge in data centre
commencements during the second half of 2025 is likely supporting activity and should remain an
important source of construction demand over the second half of this year and beyond.

### Page 9

State detail
Construction activity increased across four of the six states in Q3, although outcomes varied
considerably.
South Australia recorded the strongest quarterly increase, rising 3.9%qtr, followed by NSW (+2.2%qtr)
and Victoria (+0.8%qtr). In contrast, activity declined in Western Australia (–1.9%qtr) and Queensland
(–1.2%qtr).
Despite the quarterly volatility, construction activity remained higher than a year ago across all states.
Tasmania recorded the strongest annual growth (+17.3%yr), followed by South Australia (+11.3%yr),

NSW (+8.1%yr), Western Australia (+6.4%yr), Victoria (+4.4%yr) and Queensland (+2.6%yr).

Document 338: Hedge funds like gilts, but not the risks

Source PDF: all report pdf/Hedge funds like gilts, but not the risks.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-27
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

Hedge funds are increasingly absorbing gilt supply through
leveraged trading strategies but passing interest-rate risk to
other investors. The Bank of England is looking at policy
measures to mitigate the associated risks. Should hedge funds
exit such trades, gilts would likely underperform swaps as gilts
move to more expensive balance sheets
Michiel Tukker
Hedge funds are absorbing more gilt supply, but not the underlying interest-
rate risk, leaving yields vulnerable to further upward pressure

Gilt demand increasingly relies on more
price-sensitive buyers
The Bank of England’s moves to unwind its bond portfolio and
persistent government deﬁcits continue to increase the supply of
gilts that markets need to absorb. Meanwhile, demand from
traditional buyers such as UK pension funds has softened. Gilts
must also compete with a growing pool of sovereign debt globally,
with expanding US Treasury issuance and ﬁnancing needs tied to
the AI capex boom helping to keep global yields elevated.

### Page 2

Domestic non-bank ﬁnancials, including asset managers and
hedge funds, now account for almost 20% of gilt holdings, up from
just 5% in 2022. These investors tend to be much more price-
sensitive than other buyers and only step in when yields are
deemed attractive. Pension funds used to be important buyers of
gilts to match their long-dated liabilities. Their role is shrinking as
the sector transitions from deﬁned beneﬁt to deﬁned contribution
schemes.

Price-sensitive buyers have had to step in
to absorb QT supply
Hedge funds are getting paid for balance
sheet, not for interest rate risk
Hedge funds have shifted from big sellers to big buyers of gilts, but
their strategies appear designed to avoid the associated interest-
rate risk. Since quantitative tightening began, hedge funds have
begun holding signiﬁcant long positions in gilts. Most of this
exposure can likely be explained by futures-cash basis trades.

Unlike the gilt scarcity we saw during quantitative easing, the gilt
market is now ﬂooded with supply. This supply pressure widens the
spread between the pricing of futures and cash gilts. A simpliﬁed
explanation is that cash gilts take up much more balance sheet
capacity compared to gilt futures. As a result, many pension funds,
banks, and asset managers prefer futures to manage interest rate

exposure, even if implied yields are lower. In practice, the diﬀerence
can be as little as a few basis points.

seller to a buyer of gilts

Hedge funds face fewer balance sheet or regulatory constraints,
allowing them to exploit the pricing diﬀerence between cash gilts

and gilt futures. The trade is simple: buy cash gilts and short gilt
futures. Repo markets oﬀer the leverage to scale this trade, with
repo dealers lending cash against gilt collateral. BoE data highlights
a parallel increase in futures positions alongside rising repo activity.
The futures-cash basis trade relies on long
gilt and short future positions

The catch is that by hedging with short futures positions, hedge
funds aren’t actually absorbing the gilt market’s underlying

### Page 4

interest-rate risk.
Instead, the long futures positions are almost entirely held by asset
managers, including pension funds. For pension funds, much of this
exposure stems from regulatory requirements and the need to
match liabilities. But outside the pension and insurance sectors,
asset managers are likely to be quite price-sensitive in their futures
exposure.
Hedge funds pass the interest rate risk on
to other ﬁnancials
Hedge funds might therefore help to keep gilt spreads versus

futures (and swaps) tight by using their balance sheets to absorb
gilt supply. But buyers are still needed to absorb the market’s
underlying duration risk. That leaves us cautious that the term
premium embedded in the sterling curve, whether expressed
through gilts or swaps, could keep rising as increasing supply tests
the market’s capacity to absorb it.
Hedge funds have increased their footprint
in the eurozone and US too
In the euro area, hedge funds’ average net repo borrowing against

government bonds stood at around €70 billion this year (c.1% of
the government bond market), below the roughly £80 billion
recorded in the gilt market (c.3% of the gilt market). The European
Central Bank noted a signiﬁcant unwinding of hedge fund positions

### Page 5

in March when the Iran conﬂict triggered a rise in rate volatility. This
also coincided with a signiﬁcant widening of European government
bond (EGB) spreads. Since then, the volatility in EGB spreads has
remained elevated, although cause and eﬀect are diﬃcult to
pinpoint. In any case, the ECB has recently identiﬁed hedge funds'
material role in EGB trades as a potential ﬁnancial stability risk.
The US Treasury futures basis trade has also been a recurring focus

of the Federal Reserve’s ﬁnancial stability assessments. The latest
estimate suggests the futures basis trade accounts for a total of
$830bn of hedge funds' US Treasury positions (also around 3% of
the market). Part of the sharp sell-oﬀ in US Treasuries in March 2020
has been attributed to the unwinding of basis trades. While the
precise impact on yields is diﬃcult to quantify, the substantial
growth in these positions since then has increased the potential for

ﬁnancial stability risks.
Headwinds to the basis trade can trigger a
sell-off in government bonds
Because hedge funds provide substantial balance-sheet capacity
and liquidity to bond markets, unwinding these positions risks
putting upward pressure on yields. Other investors are unlikely to
step in unless bond valuations become suﬃciently attractive.
Banks, for example, face high balance sheet costs, and potentially
leverage ratio constraints, limiting their appetite. In eﬀect, we would

expect gilts to underperform swaps in such a scenario, pushing gilt
yields higher.
Leveraged gilt positions that rely on repo markets could face
headwinds if system liquidity tightens. Higher repo rates make
funding more expensive and could reduce the attractiveness of
speculative positions. Higher regulatory haircuts could also force
unwinding of leverage as the trade becomes less attractive.
Another risk stems from sharp moves in interest rates. A sudden

rise in yields could trigger margin calls on hedge funds’ short
futures positions, forcing them to liquidate gilt holdings. The
dynamics would resemble the 2022 gilt crisis, when an unfunded
mini-budget ﬂoated by Prime Minister Liz Truss’ government

triggered market turmoil, widespread margin calls, and forced
selling by pension funds.
Since then, the BoE has strengthened liquidity backstops for
pension funds, reducing the risk of a repeat episode. Hedge funds,
however, have less direct access to such support mechanisms. That
said, as it did during the 2022 gilt crisis, the Bank could step in and
purchase gilts in the secondary market to prevent a disorderly
unwind.
Another complexity is that hedge funds often pursue multiple

strategies, so turmoil in other markets could spill over into gilts. If a
hedge fund faces losses or margin calls on an unrelated strategy, a
derisking of portfolios might also force an unwinding of gilt-related
trades.
Increased market volatility could trigger an
unwinding of hedge fund positions

The BoE is currently reviewing possible regulation to mitigate the
risks surrounding leveraged gilt trades, which could make basis
trades less attractive. For example, imposing minimum haircuts on
bilateral trades would reduce the leverage hedge funds can obtain.
Alternatively, requiring more central clearing in repo markets could

help reduce counterparty credit risk. But this could also increase
margining costs for hedge funds.

ANALYST: Tajinder Dhillon, CFA October 30, 2026 
tajinder.dhillon@lseg.com; 
THIS WEEK IN EARNINGS 
AGGREGATE ESTIMATES AND REVISIONS 
 26Q3 Y/Y earnings are expected to be 31.6%. Excluding the energy sector, the Y/Y earnings estimate is 26.4%. 
 Of the 299 companies in the S&P 500 that have reported earnings to date for 26Q3, 81.6% have reported earnings 
above analyst estimates. This compares to a long-term average of 67.7% and prior four quarter average of 81.5%. 
 26Q3 Y/Y revenue is expected to be 13.6%. Excluding the energy sector, the growth estimate is 12.2%. 
 69.9% of companies have reported 26Q3 revenue above analyst expectations. This compares to a long-term 
average of 63% and an average over the past four quarters of 76.8%. 
 For 26Q3, there have been 40 negative EPS preannouncements issued by S&P 500 corporations compared to 66 
positive EPS preannouncements. By dividing 40 by 66 the N/P ratio is 0.6 for the S&P 500 Index.  
 The forward four-quarter (26Q4– 27Q3) P/E ratio for the S&P 500 is 19.0. 
 During the week of Nov. 2, 144 S&P 500 companies are expected to report quarterly earnings.

There has been an increase in the share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 since the start of the quarter (to $778.4B 
from $760.7B). Three of the eleven sectors have experienced downward revisions to estimates.

Since Aug. 1, the communication services (-20.5%) and materials (-7%) sectors have recorded the highest percentage 
decreases in earnings, while the energy (33%) sector has recorded the highest percentage increase in earnings. 
Overall, share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 have increased by 2.3% since the start of the quarter.

Since Aug. 1, the communication services (-14.7B) and materials (-1.2B) sectors have recorded the highest dollar-level 
decreases in earnings, while the energy (15.7B) sector has recorded the highest dollar-level increase in earnings. 
Overall, expected share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 have increased by 17.7B since the start of the quarter.

299 companies in the S&P 500 Index have reported earnings for Q3 2026. Of these companies, 81.6% reported 
earnings above analyst expectations and 15.7% reported earnings below analyst expectations. In a typical quarter 
(since 1994), 67% of companies beat estimates and 20% miss estimates. Over the past four quarters, 82% of 
companies beat the estimates and 15% missed estimates. 
In aggregate, companies are reporting earnings that are 4.5% above estimates, which compares to a long-term (since 
1994) average surprise factor of 4.4% and the average surprise factor over the prior four quarters of 7.7%.

The estimated earnings growth rate for the S&P 500 for 26Q3 is 31.6%. If the energy sector is excluded, the growth 
rate declines to 26.4%. The S&P 500 expects to see share-weighted earnings of $778.4B in 26Q3, compared to share-
weighted earnings of $591.4B (based on the year-ago earnings of the current 503 constituents) in 25Q3. 
11 of the 11 sectors in the index expect to see an improvement in earnings relative to 25Q3. The Energy and 
Information Technology sectors have the highest earnings growth rates for the quarter, while the Consumer 
Discretionary sector has the weakest anticipated growth compared to 25Q3. 
The Energy sector has the highest earnings growth rate (146.2%) of any sector. It is expected to earn $63B in 26Q3, 
compared to earnings of $25.6B in 25Q3. 4 of the 5 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to see higher earnings 
than a year ago. The Oil & Gas Refining & Marketing (566.5%) and Integrated Oil & Gas (143.8%) sub-industries have 
the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate declines to 39%. 
The Information Technology sector has the second highest earnings growth rate (68.9%) of any sector. It is expected 
to earn $260.2B in 26Q3, compared to earnings of $154B in 25Q3. 12 of the 12 sub-industries in the sector are 
anticipated to see higher earnings than a year ago. The Semiconductors (137%) and Semiconductor Materials & 
Equipment (71.5%) sub-industries have the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, 
the growth rate declines to 28.2%. 
The Consumer Discretionary sector has the lowest earnings growth rate (3.9%) of any sector. It is expected to earn 
$56.3B in 26Q3, compared to earnings of $54.2B in 25Q3. 7 of the 18 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to 
see lower earnings than a year ago. The Casinos & Gaming (-44.1%) and Automotive Parts & Equipment (-38.8%) 
sub-industries have the lowest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate 
improves to 4.5%.

The estimated earnings growth rate for the S&P 500 for 26Q4 is 29.7%. If the energy sector is excluded, the growth 
rate declines to 25%. The S&P 500 expects to see share-weighted earnings of $810.7B in 26Q4, compared to share-
weighted earnings of $625.1B (based on the year-ago earnings of the current 503 constituents) in 25Q4. 
11 of the 11 sectors in the index expect to see an improvement in earnings relative to 25Q4. The Energy and 
Information Technology sectors have the highest earnings growth rates for the quarter, while the Industrials sector has 
the weakest anticipated growth compared to 25Q4. 
The Energy sector has the highest earnings growth rate (145.6%) of any sector. It is expected to earn $59.5B in 26Q4, 
compared to earnings of $24.2B in 25Q4. 3 of the 5 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to see higher earnings 
than a year ago. The Oil & Gas Refining & Marketing (366.3%) and Integrated Oil & Gas (175.7%) sub-industries have 
the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate declines to 52.1%. 
The Information Technology sector has the second highest earnings growth rate (56.3%) of any sector. It is expected 
to earn $292.1B in 26Q4, compared to earnings of $186.8B in 25Q4. 12 of the 12 sub-industries in the sector are 
anticipated to see higher earnings than a year ago. The Semiconductors (119.5%) and Semiconductor Materials & 
Equipment (72.3%) sub-industries have the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, 
the growth rate declines to 19.1%. 
The Industrials sector has the lowest earnings growth rate (3.9%) of any sector. It is expected to earn $52B in 26Q4, 
compared to earnings of $50.1B in 25Q4. 2 of the 20 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to see lower earnings 
than a year ago. The Aerospace & Defense (-39.6%) and Passenger Airlines (-29.8%) sub-industries have the lowest 
earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate improves to 30.5%.

STARMINE EARNINGS SURPRISE FORECAST
Looking forward at quarterly performance, we use StarMine’s SmartEstimate® from LSEG to determine which
companies in the S&P 500 are better poised to beat and miss earnings estimates. The SmartEstimate® is a weighted

average of analyst estimates, with more weight given to more recent estimates and more accurate analysts. Our
studies have shown that when the SmartEstimate® differs significantly from the consensus (I/B/E/S mean estimate),
the Predicted Surprise accurately predicts the direction of earnings surprises or further revisions 70% of the time.
When a significant Predicted Surprise for revenue is also present for the period, the accuracy improves to 78%.

StarMine ARM from LSEG is an analyst revisions stock ranking model, designed to predict future changes in analyst
sentiment. ARM incorporates more accurate earnings estimates through the SmartEstimate prediction service. ARM
region rankings scores companies by region on a scale of 1 to 100 where 100 represents the most bullish sentiment.
Over the next two weeks, 147 S&P 500 companies are expected to report earnings. Of these companies, 17 positive

surprises and 11 negative surprises are expected from S&P 500 companies reporting quarterly results.

Document 362: Minutes of ECB’s October meeting confirm tightening bias

Source PDF: all report pdf/Minutes of ECB’s October meeting confirm tightening bias.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-26
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

The minutes of the ECB's October meeting conﬁrm a clear
tightening bias and suggest a rate hike at the December
meeting is almost a done deal
Carsten Brzeski
ECB President Christine Lagarde
The just-released minutes of the ECB’s October meeting conﬁrmed
the central bank's current tightening bias. Given that many ECB
members had already been in favour of a rate hike at the October
meeting, a December hike looks almost like a done deal. On a
slightly diﬀerent note, the minutes also shed new light on the ECB’s

internal arithmetic: a large number of members can still fall short of
a majority.
Here are the most important passages from the minutes:
Waiting until December to get a better picture of the longer-term
inﬂation forecast. "The December projections, which would extend
the forecast horizon to include 2029, would provide an updated
integrated assessment of how the energy shock, AI-related

our Cookie policy.

Targeting Cookies
Save preferences Accept all

### Page 2

dynamics, the shift in ﬁnancing conditions and all other relevant
developments were shaping the inﬂation outlook and the
surrounding risks."
Concerns about the growth outlook. "Looking ahead, members
generally agreed that tightening ﬁnancial conditions and the
renewed spike in energy prices would have a negative eﬀect on
growth, although its magnitude remained to be seen. The decline in
demand for loans suggested a stronger adverse eﬀect on activity

than had been seen so far, although some of the eﬀect from higher
rates on private investment spending, housing and consumption
might be attenuated by the weaker exchange rate."
Inﬂation remains primarily an energy price shock (for now).
"Members concurred with the assessment presented by Philip Lane
in his introduction, noting that, on the one hand, energy prices had
increased beyond the baseline path foreseen in the September ECB
staﬀ projections, which was likely to raise inﬂation further in the

near term and keep headline inﬂation well above target over the
coming year. On the other hand, underlying inﬂation had remained
broadly stable in recent months and wage growth data had
moderated further."
Some concerns about sticky inﬂation. "Regarding the near-term
outlook, on the one hand, it was clear that inﬂation would be
higher for longer than previously expected. On the other hand, the
latest data for inﬂation in the HICP excluding energy and food

suggested a slightly better outlook than previously expected, but
allowing for pass-through from higher energy prices would suggest
a higher core inﬂation trajectory at or above the level seen in the
adverse scenario. It was suggested that perhaps underlying
inﬂation could become sticky at around 2.5% rather than receding."
Not giving up on those indirect eﬀects. "Regarding the direct,
indirect and second-round eﬀects from higher energy prices, thus

far it was mostly direct eﬀects that were materialising. Indirect
eﬀects had overall remained limited, as expected, and so far there
were no material signs of second-round eﬀects. However, the fact
that energy prices had risen even higher and for longer, together

### Page 3

with the signals from input prices and selling price expectations,
suggested that indirect eﬀects would become stronger over time
and some second-round eﬀects would eventually materialise, as
already anticipated to a degree in the September projections."
A large number is not a majority. "A large number of members
noted that, as the incoming data since the September Governing
Council meeting had conﬁrmed the case for further policy
tightening, they could have supported raising interest rates at the

current meeting. These members stressed that a further rate hike
was warranted in light of the deteriorating inﬂation outlook, while
acknowledging elevated uncertainty, the impact of higher yields on
ﬁnancial conditions and the limited evidence so far of second-
round eﬀects."
All in all, a clear conﬁrmation of the ECB's tightening bias, based on
the assumption that high energy prices will eventually still translate
into broader inﬂationary pressure; a development that is not yet

the case.
Preparing for a December hike
Today's minutes conﬁrmed ECB President Christine Lagarde’s
message at the October press conference. The fact that several ECB
members openly advocated a rate hike, even if they fell short of a
majority, sends a clear signal. If the macro backdrop at the
upcoming December meeting (on 18 December) is similar to the
one in October, a rate hike is a done deal. The only limitations to
this ‘done deal’ scenario we have heard since then, at least from the

doves, are that oil prices and ﬁnancing conditions could move
quickly, potentially in a direction making another hike obsolete.
Looking at these external circumstances, oil prices have indeed
fallen signiﬁcantly since the ECB’s October meeting but are not
much lower than when the September staﬀ projections were
conducted. As regards ﬁnancing conditions, the drop in bond yields
since September will bring little respite for ECB doves. Last but not
least, the latest weakening of the euro will probably enter the ECB’s

radar screen again these days. Since the September projections, the
nominal eﬀective exchange rate has lost some 3%. Adding all these

elements together, there will be very little reason to give the ‘all
clear’ on inﬂation.
Let’s stick with the next round of ECB staﬀ projections for a minute.

Next to changes in the so-called technical assumptions, the most
interesting elements of the forecasts will be the forecasts for wage
growth and core inﬂation. Up to now, the ECB seems to stick to the
traditional approach that higher energy prices will eventually
translate into higher core inﬂation and wage growth. Even if there
haven’t been any indications of such developments so far.
When it comes to wage growth forecasts, in particular, the ECB has

been much more optimistic than, for example, the Survey of
Professional Forecasters. In our view, ﬁnal demand remains under
too much stress from higher energy prices and labour market
insecurity to eventually see a signiﬁcant increase in core inﬂation.
Instead, corporate proﬁt margin squeezing will be the new game in
town. So far, the ECB has had a diﬀerent view. Let’s see whether this
could change at the December meeting.
Today's minutes as well as recent oﬃcial comments conﬁrm our

view that the ECB will hike again by 25bp at the December meeting.
Beyond the December meeting, however, the path for monetary
policy is less obvious. In fact, it will depend on whether the still-not-
visible impact of higher energy prices on the rest of the economy
eventually materialises. We are less convinced than the ECB and
therefore think that additional rate hikes in 2027 would do more
harm than good.

Document 388: TCT Cảng Hàng không Việt Nam - Báo cáo cập nhật 3Q2026 (2026-11-19)

Source PDF: all report pdf/TCT Cảng Hàng không Việt Nam - Báo cáo cập nhật 3Q2026 (2026-11-19).pdf
Report source: KBSV (Company)
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

TCT Cảng Hàng không Việt Nam (ACV) 
Áp lực lợi nhuận trong chu kỳ mở rộng công suất 
19/11/2026

KQKD 3Q2026 suy giảm do khấu hao 
và biến động tỷ giá

Lượt khách ACV dự kiến tăng chậm 
hơn toàn ngành do tác động từ Phú 
Quốc và Gia Bình

Long Thành dự kiến khai thác từ cuối 
2026, tạo áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận 
giai đoạn đầu

Duy trì khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, giá 
mục tiêu 39,500 VND/cổ phiếu

3Q2026, ACV ghi nhận doanh thu đạt 6,066 tỷ VND (-6% yoy) và LNST công ty mẹ 
đạt 2,206 tỷ VND (-14% yoy). Lợi nhuận suy giảm chủ yếu do (1) khấu hao tăng từ 
các nhà ga mới và (2) ghi nhận lỗ tỷ giá 168 tỷ VND trong kỳ, trái ngược với khoản lãi 
tỷ giá cùng kỳ 2025. Lũy kế 9M2026, doanh thu và LNST công ty mẹ lần lượt đạt 
19,226/7,435 tỷ VND (+0%/+2% yoy).

Chúng tôi dự phóng lượt khách quốc tế/nội địa qua hệ thống ACV tăng trưởng bình 
quân 7%/5.5%/năm đến 2030, thấp hơn toàn ngành do (1) sân bay Phú Quốc 
không còn thuộc hệ thống ACV từ đầu 2026 và (2) một phần khách quốc tế tại Nội 
Bài dịch chuyển tự nhiên sang sân bay Gia Bình khi đi vào khai thác. Mức độ dịch 
chuyển trước năm 2030 dự kiến còn hạn chế do phụ thuộc vào tiến độ hoàn thiện 
sân bay và hạ tầng giao thông kết nối.

Cảng HKQT Long Thành giai đoạn 1 dự kiến khai thác một phần từ cuối tháng 
12/2026 và bắt đầu tiếp nhận các chuyến bay quốc tế đường dài từ Tân Sơn Nhất 
vào ngày 28/03/2027, bổ sung công suất 25 triệu khách/năm cho ACV. Tuy nhiên, 
tốc độ chuyển dịch có thể chậm hơn kế hoạch do hạ tầng kết nối chưa hoàn thiện, 
trong khi chi phí khấu hao và vận hành tăng dự kiến gây áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận 
của ACV giai đoạn 2027–2028.

Sử dụng phương pháp chiết khấu dòng tiền, chúng tôi duy trì khuyến nghị TRUNG 
LẬP đối với ACV với giá mục tiêu 39,500 VND/cổ phiếu, tương đương tiềm năng tăng 
giá 4.5% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 18/11/2026.

### Page 2

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2025 Hoạt động kinh doanh

ACV hiện đang quản lý, đầu tư và khai thác 
hệ thống 21 Cảng hàng không trong cả nước, 
bao gồm 10 Cảng hàng không quốc tế và 11 
Cảng hàng không nội địa. Ngành nghề kinh 
doanh chính của ACV là hoạt động cung cấp 
dịch vụ hỗ trợ trực tiếp cho vận tải hàng 
không, bao gồm dịch vụ hàng không, dịch vụ 
phi hàng không và bán hàng. 
Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Tăng trưởng bền vững đến từ cả nhóm khách nội địa và quốc tế, tốc độ tăng trung bình dự kiến đạt 
5.5% và 7%/năm giai đoạn 2026-2030. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Long Thành bổ sung 25 triệu khách/năm từ 2027, song khấu hao tăng mạnh khiến lợi nhuận chạm 
đáy năm 2028 trước khi phục hồi từ 2029. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Quá trình lên HOSE của ACV tiến triển thuận lợi nhờ các tài sản nhà nước đang dần được tách bạch, 
tạo thuận lợi cho việc hoàn tất cổ phần hóa tài sản khu bay cho phần vốn nhà nước. 
 Tham khảo trong báo cáo kỳ trước tại đây

hao và biến động tỷ giá

3Q2026, ACV ghi nhận doanh thu đạt 6,066 tỷ VND (-6% yoy) và LNST công ty

tăng từ các nhà ga mới và (2) ghi nhận lỗ tỷ giá 168 tỷ VND trong kỳ, trái ngược 
với khoản lãi tỷ giá cùng kỳ 2025. Lũy kế 9M2026, doanh thu và LNST công ty 
mẹ lần lượt đạt 19,226/7,435 tỷ VND (+0%/+2% yoy).

Biểu đồ 6. Các quốc gia có lượng khách đến Việt Nam lớn nhất trong 10M2026 (khách) 
Trung Quốc và Hàn Quốc tiếp tục là hai thị 
trường khách quốc tế lớn nhất đến Việt Nam 
trong 10M2026 nhưng tăng trưởng hạn chế 
(+1.4% và -6.3% yoy), chủ yếu chịu tác động 
từ giá nhiên liệu tăng cao, khiến các hãng 
hàng không cắt giảm tần suất bay và du 
khách có xu hướng ưu tiên các điểm đến gần. 
Ngược lại, khách châu Âu tăng mạnh (+51.5% 
yoy), chủ yếu nhờ (1) thị trường Nga phục hồi 
(+150% yoy) sau khi các đường bay thẳng và 
charter được khôi phục, mở rộng và (2) hưởng 
lợi từ chính sách miễn thị thực bổ sung cho 12 
quốc gia châu Âu từ tháng 8/2025 và việc tăng 
cường kết nối hàng không trong năm 2026.

Nguồn: GSO

Triển vọng tăng trưởng hành khách 
tích cực, song ACV dự kiến tăng 
chậm hơn toàn ngành 
 Theo dự báo của Bộ Xây dựng, lượng hành khách thông qua các cảng hàng 
không Việt Nam dự kiến tăng trưởng bình quân 9.7%/năm giai đoạn 2026–
2030, đạt 191.2 triệu lượt vào năm 2030. Về dài hạn, Airbus dự báo lưu lượng 
hành khách hàng không Việt Nam tăng trưởng 7.7%/năm trong 20 năm tới, gần 
gấp khoảng 2 lần mức trung bình toàn cầu (3.9%/năm). Các động lực chính bao 
gồm triển vọng tăng trưởng kinh tế, nhu cầu du lịch gia tăng, chính sách miễn 
thị thực thuận lợi và việc mở rộng mạng đường bay quốc tế, cùng kế hoạch bổ 
sung đội bay của các hãng hàng không.

### Page 5

Chúng tôi dự phóng lượng khách quốc tế và nội địa của ACV tăng trưởng bình 
quân lần lượt 7% và 5.5%/năm đến 2030, thấp hơn mức tăng trưởng chung của 
toàn ngành do (1) sân bay Phú Quốc được chuyển ra khỏi hệ thống ACV từ đầu 
2026 và (2) một phần khách quốc tế tại Nội Bài dịch chuyển tự nhiên sang Gia 
Bình khi sân bay này đi vào khai thác. Chúng tôi đánh giá mức độ dịch chuyển 
trước năm 2030 còn hạn chế do phụ thuộc vào tiến độ xây dựng sân bay và hạ 
tầng kết nối. Bên cạnh đó, đề xuất chuyển các chuyến bay quốc tế từ Nội Bài 
sang Gia Bình hiện chưa được phê duyệt và chưa được phản ánh trong dự 
phóng. Nếu được thông qua, đề xuất này có thể ảnh hưởng đáng kể đến lợi 
nhuận của ACV, do lợi nhuận trên mỗi khách quốc tế cao gấp khoảng 4–5 lần 
khách nội địa, đặc biệt trong giai đoạn sau năm 2030.

Cảng HKQT Long Thành mở ra dư 
địa tăng trưởng dài hạn, song tạo 
áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận trong 
ngắn và trung hạn 
 Cảng HKQT Long Thành dự kiến khai thác một phần từ cuối tháng 12/2026, chủ 
yếu phục vụ các chuyến bay nội địa và một số chuyến quốc tế. Hoạt động 
chuyển các chuyến bay quốc tế đường dài và vận tải hàng hóa từ Tân Sơn Nhất 
dự kiến bắt đầu từ ngày 28/03/2027. Tuy nhiên, chúng tôi đánh giá tốc độ dịch 
chuyển có thể chậm hơn kế hoạch do phụ thuộc vào tiến độ hoàn thiện hạ tầng 
giao thông kết nối với trung tâm TP.HCM, đặc biệt là dự án mở rộng cao tốc 
TP.HCM – Long Thành – Dầu Giây và các dự án kết nối đường sắt bao gồm Thủ 
Thiêm – Long Thành.

Dự án cảng HKQT Long Thành kỳ vọng sẽ mở ra dư địa tăng trưởng dài hạn cho 
ACV, dù gây áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận trong giai đoạn đầu khai thác. Việc đưa 
Long Thành vào vận hành sẽ giúp ACV mở rộng công suất, giảm áp lực quá tải 
tại Tân Sơn Nhất, đồng thời tạo điều kiện thu hút thêm các đường bay quốc tế, 
đặc biệt là đường bay dài và hoạt động vận tải hàng hóa. Tuy nhiên, trong ngắn 
và trung hạn, chi phí khấu hao và vận hành tăng trong khi sản lượng hành 
khách cần thời gian để đạt công suất tối ưu dự kiến sẽ gây áp lực lên biên lợi 
nhuận của ACV giai đoạn 2027–2028. Trong mô hình dự phóng, chúng tôi ghi 
nhận khấu hao Long Thành theo tiến độ nghiệm thu, kết chuyển và đưa vào sử 
dụng từng hạng mục tài sản cố định, thay vì toàn bộ dự án ngay khi bắt đầu vận 
hành.

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, 
giá mục tiêu 39,500 VND/cổ phiếu  
 Sử dụng phương pháp chiết khấu dòng tiền, chúng tôi duy trì khuyến nghị 
TRUNG LẬP đối với cổ phiếu ACV trong bối cảnh rủi ro từ đề xuất chuyển các 
chuyến bay quốc tế từ Nội Bài sang Gia Bình gia tăng. Giá mục tiêu của ACV là 
39,500 VND/cổ phiếu, tương đương với mức tăng giá tiềm năng 4.5% so với mức 
giá đóng cửa 37,800 VND/cổ phiếu ngày 18/11/2026. Các giả định chính bao 
gồm: 
- Giai đoạn 2026–2030, dự phóng lượt khách quốc tế/nội địa qua hệ 
thống cảng hàng không ACV tăng trưởng bình quân 7%/5.5%/năm, 
thấp hơn tốc độ tăng trưởng chung của ngành hàng không Việt Nam, 
chủ yếu do (1) ACV không còn ghi nhận sản lượng hành khách tại sân 
bay Phú Quốc và (2) giả định một phần khách quốc tế tại Nội Bài dịch 
chuyển tự nhiên sang Gia Bình với mức độ còn hạn chế do hạ tầng kết 
nối chưa hoàn thiện. Dự phóng chưa phản ánh đề xuất chuyển các 
chuyến bay quốc tế sang Gia Bình. 
- Giá dịch vụ trung bình cảng hàng không dự kiến sẽ điều chỉnh tăng 2% 
mỗi 4 năm. 
- Dự án Long Thành giai đoạn 1 và giai đoạn 2 bắt đầu đưa vào khai thác 
từ cuối 2026 và đầu 2032, gia tăng doanh thu nhưng đồng thời tạo áp 
lực lên biên lợi nhuận trong giai đoạn đầu vận hành do chi phí khấu 
hao và vận hành tăng.

### Page 8

Rủi ro giảm đối với giá mục tiêu ACV chủ yếu đến từ khả năng đề xuất chuyển 
các chuyến bay quốc tế từ Nội Bài sang Gia Bình được phê duyệt. Kịch bản cơ 
sở hiện chỉ phản ánh mức dịch chuyển tự nhiên. Trong trường hợp đề xuất 
được thông qua, sản lượng khách quốc tế qua hệ thống ACV có thể thấp hơn 
đáng kể so với dự phóng, kéo theo suy giảm doanh thu và lợi nhuận do khả 
năng sinh lời trên mỗi khách quốc tế cao hơn khoảng 4–5 lần so với khách nội 
địa.

Biểu đồ 13. P/B, ROE lịch sử

P/B của ACV tiếp tục giảm về quanh 1.8–1.9x, 
chạm và có lúc xuống dưới ngưỡng -2std. Đây 
là vùng thấp nhất 5 năm và chiết khấu sâu so 
với mức trung bình 5 năm, phản ánh lo ngại 
về áp lực khấu hao và chi phí khi Long Thành 
đi vào vận hành.

ASIA MONTHLY 2026 年 11 月（No.308）
JAPAN RESEARCH INSTITUTE
1
保険業界の規制改革とインド経済
インド政府は、過去四半世紀にわたり国民生活の安定と金融仲介機能の強化に向けて保険市場の自由化を
推進してきたが、ここにきて消費者保護強化に向けた規制強化にかじを切ろうとしている。
■規制緩和を受けてインドの保険市場は急成長
インド政府は、国民生活の安定と金融仲介機
能の強化に向けて、保険市場の発展を推進して
きた。この背景としては、以下の 2 点を指摘で
きる。
第 1 に、インドでは社会保障制度が整備途上
にある。そのため、予期せぬ疾病や事故をきっ
かけとする世帯所得の急減リスクを抑制するう
えで、医療保険や生命保険への加入が極めて重
要なセーフティネットとなる。
第 2 に、持続的なインド経済の発展のために
は、預入期間の短い銀行預金とは別に、インフ
ラ整備や産業育成の原動力となる長期かつ安定
的な国内資本の蓄積が必要である。保険会社が

集めて運用する巨額の資金は、まさにこの長期
資金供給の担い手として期待されてきた。
これらの目的の達成に向け、インド政府は過
去四半世紀にわたって段階的な規制緩和を進め
てきた（右上表）。2000 年の民間企業や外資企
業の参入自由化を皮切りに、2002 年のバンカシ
ュアランス（銀行窓口での保険販売）解禁、外
資出資比率上限の引き上げなどを進めた。
こうした一連の規制緩和によって多数の民
間・外国企業が参入し、市場競争が活性化した。
日本からも日本生命や東京海上グループを含め、
複数の保険会社が参入し、対印投資を牽引した。
市場参入や販売に関わる規制緩和に加え、中
間所得層の拡大、家計における金融リテラシー
の向上、そして保険サービスのデジタル化も相
まって、インドの保険市場は飛躍的な拡大を遂
げた。保険料収入は経済成長率を上回るペースで拡大し、近年の保険会社の運用資産残高は GDP
の約 4 分の 1 に達している（右下図）。
インド政府は、独立 100 周年を迎える 2047 年までに全国民が自らのリスクに適した保険に手

ごろな価格でアクセスできる「Insurance for All by 2047」の実現を目標に掲げており、今後もイ
ンドの保険市場は基本的に底堅い成長をたどると見込まれる。
上席主任研究員 熊谷 章太郎
(kumagai.shotaro@jri.co.jp）

時期 出来事
IRDAI（保険規制開発庁）発足
地場⺠間企業や外資の参⼊受付開始（外資の
出資上限は26％）
2002年 バンカシュアランス（銀⾏や銀⾏の所有する販売経 路を通じた保険販売）解禁
2015年 外資の出資上限を 26% から 49% へ引き上げ
2016年
新たな保険商品を販売する際の手続きにおける事
後届出制の導⼊開始（従来は事前承認制を採
用）
2021年 外資の出資上限を49% から74% へ引き上げ

2022年 オンラインおよび多様な流通チャネルに関する保険 販売規制の合理化
2023年 保険会社が代理店や中間業者に⽀払う手数料の 上限を撤廃
2025年 外資の出資上限を74％から100% へ引き上げ
2026年 IRDAI、保険販売制度の⼤規模改⾰に向けた公 開協議文書を公表

2000年
＜インドの保険業界に関する規制動向＞

### Page 3

ASIA MONTHLY 2026 年 11 月（No.308）
JAPAN RESEARCH INSTITUTE
2
■当局が規制強化に舵を切る理由
一方で、急激な市場拡大の副作用として、構造
的な問題も顕在化している。最大の課題は保険販
売コストの高騰と不適切販売の横行である。保険
販売競争が激化するなか、保険会社が商業銀行や
ノンバンクといった販売代理店に対して支払う
手数料・報酬は、新規契約保険料を大幅に上回る
ペースで増加しており（右上図）、それが新規保
険料の上昇や将来の保険料支払いの減少につな
がることへの懸念が広がった。また、顧客のニー
ズに合致しない複雑な商品の強引な販売や、商品
に関する説明不足も社会問題化した。
こうした状況を重くみた IRDAI（イン
ド保険規制開発局）は、市場の健全性を
維持すべく、足元で消費者保護を主眼と
した規制強化を検討している。2026 年 9
月に公表した改革案では、①販売代理店
への報酬構造の見直し、②保険会社の事
業費・管理費に対する規制厳格化、③販

売手法に対する規制厳格化、④コンプラ
イアンスの徹底に向けた流通チャネルの
合理化、⑤監査や情報公開制度の厳格化、
などの方向性が示された（右下表）。改
革案に関する意見を広く募ったうえで、
2027 年前半に新たな制度を導入するとし
ている。
一連の規制強化の影響は、プラス面と
マイナス面に分けられる。まず、プラス
面としては、過度なインセンティブ競争
や不適切な販売が抑制されることで消費
者保護が大幅に強化され、保険市場の信
頼性と持続的発展の基盤が強化される点が挙げられる。
他方、マイナス面として懸念されるのは、移行期における副作用である。販売規制の厳格化に
伴い、保険会社および販売代理店である金融機関の収益拡大ペースの一時的な鈍化は避けられな
い。さらに、保険会社や金融機関がインドの金融市場における主たる投資主体であることを勘案
すると、規制強化が株式・債券価格に対して下押し圧力をもたらす公算が大きい。
保険市場のこうした変化は、今後のインド経済を展望するうえで重要な視点を提供する。すな

わち、保険業界に限らず多くの産業において、自由化による市場の急成長は様々な問題を生じさ
せ得るものの、そうした事態に対する規制の再設計・適正化こそが、新たな発展の礎になるとい
う点である。その嚆矢として保険をめぐる規制の行方が注目される。
